    Accent = {114, 137, 218},
    Transparency = 0,
    Scale = 1,
    ToggleKey = "RightShift",
    Notifications = true,

    Language = nil,
    LightTheme = false,
}

pcall(function()
    if isfile and readfile and isfile(SL.CONFIG_FILE) then
        local data = SL.HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(SL.CONFIG_FILE))
        for k, v in pairs(data) do SL.Config[k] = v end
    end
end)

SL.Config.Binds = type(SL.Config.Binds) == "table" and SL.Config.Binds or {}

function SL.SaveConfig()
    pcall(function()
        if writefile then writefile(SL.CONFIG_FILE, SL.HttpService:JSONEncode(SL.Config)) end
    end)
end

            langPromptB2  = "English",

    else

            hubName       = "Script Loader",

            skySec        = "Небо",
            customSkyId   = "Asset ID своего неба",

-- Читаем сохранённый язык или показываем выбор
if SL.Config.Language then
    SL.SetupLang(SL.Config.Language)
else
    -- Диалог выбора языка
    -- LangGui создаётся до инициализации New() — используем Instance.new напрямую
    local function _LNew(cls, props, parent)
        local o = Instance.new(cls)
        for k,v in pairs(props or {}) do o[k]=v end
        if parent then o.Parent=parent end
        return o
    end
    local LangGui = _LNew("ScreenGui", {
        Name = "LangSelect", ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
        ResetOnSpawn = false, DisplayOrder = 1000, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
    })
    local ok = false
    pcall(function() LangGui.Parent = SL.CoreGui ok = true end)
    if not ok then LangGui.Parent = SL.PlayerGui end

    local overlay = _LNew("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8,8,12), BackgroundTransparency = 0.3,
        Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0), ZIndex = 1,
    }, LangGui)
    local card = _LNew("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22,22,30), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,300,0,160), ZIndex = 2,
    }, LangGui)
    do local c = Instance.new("UICorner", card) c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,12) end
    do local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", card) st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80,80,110) st.Thickness = 1 end
    _LNew("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency=1, Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,18), Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,28),
        Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize=16, TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),
        Text="Language / Язык", ZIndex=3,
    }, card)
    _LNew("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency=1, Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,48), Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,20),
        Font=Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize=12, TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(160,160,180),
        Text="Choose interface language / Выбери язык интерфейса", ZIndex=3,
    }, card)
    local function mkBtn(text, x, lang)
        local b = _LNew("TextButton", {
            BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(40,40,55), BorderSizePixel=0, AutoButtonColor=false,
            AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0,0), Position=UDim2.new(0,x,0,90), Size=UDim2.new(0,120,0,40),
            Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize=13, TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1), Text=text, ZIndex=3,
        }, card)
        do local c=Instance.new("UICorner",b) c.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,8) end
        b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() b.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(114,137,218) end)
        b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() b.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(40,40,55) end)
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            SL.SetupLang(lang)
            SL.Config.Language = lang
            pcall(function() if writefile then writefile(SL.CONFIG_FILE, SL.HttpService:JSONEncode(SL.Config)) end end)
            LangGui:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    mkBtn("Русский", 20, "ru")
    mkBtn("English", 160, "en")
    -- Ждём выбора
    local done = false
    repeat task.wait(0.1) done = not LangGui.Parent until done
end

-- ==================== УТИЛИТЫ ====================
SL.Conns, SL.Cleanups, SL.AccentObjects = {}, {}, {}

function SL.Add(c) table.insert(SL.Conns, c) return c end
function SL.OnUnload(fn) table.insert(SL.Cleanups, fn) end

function SL.New(class, props, parent)
    local o = Instance.new(class)
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do o[k] = v end
    if parent then o.Parent = parent end
    return o
end

function SL.Round(o, r) return SL.New("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 6)}, o) end

function SL.Tween(o, t, props, style, dir)
    local tw = SL.TweenService:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(t, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props)
    tw:Play()
    return tw
end

function SL.BindAccent(inst, prop)
    table.insert(SL.AccentObjects, {inst, prop})
    inst[prop] = SL.Theme.Accent
end

function SL.GetChar() return SL.LocalPlayer.Character end
function SL.GetHum()
    local c = SL.GetChar()
    return c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end
function SL.GetRoot()
    local c = SL.GetChar()
    return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

function SL.nearestEnemy(maxDist, teamCheck)
    local root = SL.GetRoot()
    if not root then return nil end
    local best, bd, bhum = nil, maxDist or math.huge, nil
    for _, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= SL.LocalPlayer and p.Character and not (teamCheck and p.Team ~= nil and p.Team == SL.LocalPlayer.Team) then
            local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local tr = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hum and hum.Health > 0 and tr then
                local d = (tr.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if d < bd then bd, best, bhum = d, p, hum end
            end
        end
    end
    return best, bd, bhum
end


-- Безопасный клик: на мобильных (Delta) mouse1click может крашить, поэтому там используем Tool:Activate / VirtualUser
function SL.ClickOnce()
    local c = SL.LocalPlayer.Character
    local tool = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if tool then
        pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
        return
    end
    if not SL.isMobile and mouse1click then
        pcall(mouse1click)
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        local vu = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
        vu:CaptureController()
        vu:Button1Down(Vector2.new(0, 0), workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame)
        task.wait()
        vu:Button1Up(Vector2.new(0, 0), workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame)
    end)
end

-- Трекер фоновых объектов для полной смены темы
SL.BgObjects = {}  -- {inst, prop, darkVal, lightVal}
SL.isLightTheme = false

SL.THEMES = {
    dark = {
        Background   = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 26),
        Top          = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 37),
        Sidebar      = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 31),
        Element      = Color3.fromRGB(36, 36, 47),
        ElementHover = Color3.fromRGB(48, 48, 63),
        Text         = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240),
        SubText      = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 165),
    },
    light = {
        Background   = Color3.fromRGB(240, 241, 248),
        Top          = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        Sidebar      = Color3.fromRGB(228, 230, 240),
        Element      = Color3.fromRGB(210, 213, 230),
        ElementHover = Color3.fromRGB(195, 198, 220),
        Text         = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 40),
        SubText      = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 115),
    },
}

function SL.BindBg(inst, prop, darkVal, lightVal)
    local entry = {inst, prop, darkVal, lightVal}
    table.insert(SL.BgObjects, entry)
    inst[prop] = SL.isLightTheme and lightVal or darkVal
end

function SL.ApplyFullTheme(light)
    SL.isLightTheme = light
    local t = light and SL.THEMES.light or SL.THEMES.dark
    SL.Theme.Background, SL.Theme.Top = t.Background, t.Top
    SL.Theme.Sidebar, SL.Theme.Element = t.Sidebar, t.Element
    SL.Theme.ElementHover, SL.Theme.Text = t.ElementHover, t.Text
    SL.Theme.SubText = t.SubText
    for i = #SL.BgObjects, 1, -1 do
        local e = SL.BgObjects[i]
        if e[1] and e[1].Parent then
            e[1][e[2]] = light and e[4] or e[3]
        else
            table.remove(SL.BgObjects, i)
        end
    end
end

SL.NotifyHolder = SL.New("Frame", {
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
    Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 56),
    Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 1, -70),
    ZIndex = 50,
}, SL.ScreenGui)
SL.New("UIListLayout", {
    Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
    SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,
    HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right,
}, SL.NotifyHolder)

function SL.Notify(title, text, dur, force)
    if not SL.Config.Notifications and not force then return end
    dur = dur or 3
    local holder = SL.New("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 66), ClipsDescendants = true}, SL.NotifyHolder)
    local card = SL.New("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Top, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, 20, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, holder)
    SL.Round(card, 8)
    SL.New("UIStroke", {Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 80), Thickness = 1}, card)
    local bar = SL.New("Frame", {Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -12), Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6), BorderSizePixel = 0}, card)
    SL.BindAccent(bar, "BackgroundColor3")
    SL.Round(bar, 2)
    SL.New("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 16),
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = title, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text, TextSize = 12,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
    }, card)
    SL.New("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 24), Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 38),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = text or "", TextColor3 = SL.Theme.SubText, TextSize = 11,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, TextWrapped = true,
    }, card)
    SL.Tween(card, 0.3, {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)}, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
    task.delay(dur, function()
        if card.Parent then
            SL.Tween(card, 0.25, {Position = UDim2.new(1, 20, 0, 0)})
            task.wait(0.28)
            holder:Destroy()
        end
    end)
end

-- Анимированные иконки статуса
SL.stepIcons = {"🔍","🎨","📖","🔧","🔌","✅"}
SL.stepTexts_ru = {
    "Проверка окружения...",
    "Загрузка темы оформления...",
    "Чтение конфигурации...",
    "Создание интерфейса...",
    "Подключение модулей...",
    "Готово!",
}
SL.stepTexts_en = {
    "Checking environment...",
    "Loading theme...",
    "Reading config...",
    "Building interface...",
    "Connecting modules...",
    "Done!",
}
SL.stepProgress = {0.15, 0.35, 0.55, 0.80, 0.95, 1.0}
SL.stepTimes    = {0.30, 0.28, 0.26, 0.28, 0.26, 0.18}
SL.useLang = SL.Config.Language or "ru"
SL.stepTexts = SL.useLang == "en" and SL.stepTexts_en or SL.stepTexts_ru

-- Spinning dots animation
SL.spinFrame = SL.New("Frame", {
    BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
    Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 144), Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 12), ZIndex = 103,
}, SL.LCard)
SL.spinDots = {}
for i = 1, 5 do
    local dot = SL.New("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new((i-1)/4, 0, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6), BackgroundTransparency = 0.5, ZIndex = 104,
    }, SL.spinFrame)
    SL.Round(dot, 3)
    table.insert(SL.spinDots, dot)
end
-- animate dots
SL.dotFrame = 0
SL.dotConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
    SL.dotFrame = (SL.dotFrame + dt * 3) % 5
    for i, dot in ipairs(SL.spinDots) do
        local dist = math.abs(((i-1) - SL.dotFrame) % 5)
        if dist > 2.5 then dist = 5 - dist end
        dot.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2 + (dist / 2.5) * 0.75
        local sz = 6 - dist * 1.5
        dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(sz, 2), 0, math.max(sz, 2))
    end
end)

for idx, txt in ipairs(SL.stepTexts) do
    -- иконка мигает
    SL.LStatus.Text = SL.stepIcons[idx] .. "  " .. txt
    SL.Tween(SL.LBarFill, SL.stepTimes[idx] * 0.9, {Size = UDim2.new(SL.stepProgress[idx], 0, 1, 0)})
    SL.LPercent.Text = math.floor(SL.stepProgress[idx] * 100) .. "%"
    -- пульс цвета бара на каждом шаге
    SL.Tween(SL.LBarFill, SL.stepTimes[idx] * 0.4, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.15},
        Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
    task.wait(SL.stepTimes[idx])
    SL.Tween(SL.LBarFill, 0.1, {BackgroundTransparency = 0})
end
SL.dotConn:Disconnect()
for _, dot in ipairs(SL.spinDots) do SL.Tween(dot, 0.15, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}) end
task.wait(0.15)


-- ==================== ГЛАВНОЕ ОКНО ====================

SL.New("UIStroke", {Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 80), Thickness = 1}, SL.MainFrame)
SL.MainScale = SL.New("UIScale", {Scale = SL.Config.Scale}, SL.MainFrame)

SL.TopBar = SL.New("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Top, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38)}, SL.MainFrame)
SL.BindBg(SL.TopBar, "BackgroundColor3", SL.THEMES.dark.Top, SL.THEMES.light.Top)
SL.AccentLine = SL.New("Frame", {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -2), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), BorderSizePixel = 0}, SL.TopBar)
SL.BindAccent(SL.AccentLine, "BackgroundColor3")
SL.New("UIGradient", {Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.8),
})}, SL.AccentLine)

SL.TitleLabel = SL.New("TextLabel", {
    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -130, 1, 0),
    Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "Script Loader  |  " .. SL.Author, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text,
    TextSize = 13, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
}, SL.TopBar)

function SL.TopButton(text, xOff, color)
    local b = SL.New("TextButton", {
        BackgroundColor3 = color or SL.Theme.Element, BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false,
        Position = UDim2.new(1, xOff, 0, 7), Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 24),
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = text, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text, TextSize = 13,
    }, SL.TopBar)
    SL.Round(b, 6)
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() SL.Tween(b, 0.15, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.3}) end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() SL.Tween(b, 0.15, {BackgroundTransparency = 0}) end)
    return b
end
SL.CloseBtn = SL.TopButton("×", -34, Color3.fromRGB(170, 50, 55))
SL.MinBtn = SL.TopButton("–", -64)

-- Перетаскивание (мышь + тач)
function SL.MakeDraggable(handle, target)
    local dragging, dragStart, startPos
    handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging, dragStart, startPos = true, i.Position, target.Position
            i.Changed:Connect(function()
                if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
            end)
        end
    end)
    SL.Add(SL.UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local d = (i.Position - dragStart) / math.max(SL.MainScale.Scale, 0.1)
            target.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
        end
    end))
end
SL.MakeDraggable(SL.TopBar, SL.MainFrame)

-- Тело окна
SL.Body = SL.New("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 38), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, SL.frameHeight - 38)}, SL.MainFrame)

SL.Sidebar = SL.New("ScrollingFrame", {
    BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Sidebar, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6), -- [BG]
    Size = UDim2.new(0, 126, 1, -34), ScrollBarThickness = 0,
    AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
}, SL.Body)
SL.Round(SL.Sidebar, 8)
SL.BindBg(SL.Sidebar, "BackgroundColor3", SL.THEMES.dark.Sidebar, SL.THEMES.light.Sidebar)
SL.New("UIListLayout", {Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center}, SL.Sidebar)
SL.New("UIPadding", {PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)}, SL.Sidebar)

SL.Content = SL.New("Frame", {
    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 138, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, -144, 1, -34),
}, SL.Body)

-- Поиск
SL.SearchBox = SL.New("TextBox", {
    BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Element, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26),
    Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, PlaceholderText = SL.Lang.search or "⌕  Search...", PlaceholderColor3 = SL.Theme.SubText,
    Text = "", TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text, TextSize = 11, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
}, SL.Content)
SL.Round(SL.SearchBox, 6)
SL.BindBg(SL.SearchBox, "BackgroundColor3", SL.THEMES.dark.Element, SL.THEMES.light.Element)

SL.Pages = SL.New("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 32), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -32)}, SL.Content)

-- Нижняя панель (FPS / Ping)
SL.Footer = SL.New("TextLabel", {
    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -24), Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20),
    Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = "FPS: -- | Ping: -- | Игроков: --", TextColor3 = SL.Theme.SubText,
    TextSize = 10, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
}, SL.Body)

do
    local frames, last = 0, tick()
    SL.Add(SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        frames += 1
        if tick() - last >= 0.5 then
            local fps = math.floor(frames / (tick() - last))
            frames, last = 0, tick()
            local ping = "--"
            pcall(function() ping = math.floor(SL.Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()) .. "ms" end)
            SL.Footer.Text = (SL.Lang.fpsLabel or "FPS: %d | Ping: %s | Players: %d"):format(fps, ping, #SL.Players:GetPlayers())
        end
    end))
end

-- ==================== БИБЛИОТЕКА ЭЛЕМЕНТОВ ====================
SL.Tabs, SL.TabButtons, SL.ActiveTab = {}, {}, nil
SL.Toggles = {}
SL.Listening = false
SL.Actions = {}

function SL.FetchSource(url)
    local src
    local ok = pcall(function() src = game:HttpGet(url) end)
    if ok and type(src) == "string" and #src > 0 then return src end
    ok = pcall(function() src = game:HttpGet(url, true) end)
    if ok and type(src) == "string" and #src > 0 then return src end
    local req = (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request) or http_request or request
    if req then
        local ok2, res = pcall(req, {Url = url, Method = "GET"})
        if ok2 and res and type(res.Body) == "string" and #res.Body > 0 then return res.Body end
    end
    return nil
end

-- Подменяет loadstring внутри запускаемого кода: если скачанный текст не Lua (404, HTML и т.д.),
-- вместо «attempt to call a nil value» будет настоящая причина и начало ответа сервера.
function SL.wrapEnv(fn)
    pcall(function()
        local base = (getgenv and getgenv()) or getfenv(0)
        local env = setmetatable({
            loadstring = function(src, ...)
                local f, e = loadstring(src, ...)
                if not f then
                    local head = string.gsub(string.sub(tostring(src), 1, 80), "%s+", " ")
                    error("loadstring не смог скомпилировать код: " .. tostring(e) .. " | начало ответа: " .. head, 0)
                end
                return f
            end,
        }, {__index = base, __newindex = base})
        setfenv(fn, env)
    end)
    return fn
end

function SL.RunUrl(name, url)
    SL.Notify("Загрузка", name .. "...", 1.5)
    local src = SL.FetchSource(url)
    if not src then error("не удалось скачать скрипт (ссылка недоступна?)", 0) end
    src = src:gsub("^\239\187\191", "")
    if string.find(string.sub(src, 1, 300):lower(), "^%s*<") then
        error("ссылка вернула HTML-страницу, а не Lua (скрипт удалён или заблокирован)", 0)
    end
    local fn, cerr = loadstring(src)
    if not fn then error("ошибка компиляции: " .. tostring(cerr), 0) end
    local ok, err = pcall(SL.wrapEnv(fn))
    if not ok then error(tostring(err), 0) end
    SL.Notify("Готово", name .. " запущен", 2)
end

-- Чип с биндом клавиши рядом с функцией
function SL.AttachBind(parent, id, action, pos)
    SL.Actions[id] = action
    local saved = SL.Config.Binds[id]
    local chip = SL.New("TextButton", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 37), BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = pos, Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 20),
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 9, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.SubText,
        Text = saved and string.sub(saved, 1, 6) or "—", ZIndex = 5,
    }, parent)
    SL.Round(chip, 5)
    chip.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if SL.Listening then return end
        SL.Listening = true
        chip.Text = "..."
        local c
        c = SL.UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                local n = i.KeyCode.Name
                if n == "Escape" or n == "Backspace" then
                    SL.Config.Binds[id] = nil
                    chip.Text = "—"
                else
                    SL.Config.Binds[id] = n
                    chip.Text = string.sub(n, 1, 6)
                end
                SL.SaveConfig()
                c:Disconnect()
                task.delay(0.2, function() SL.Listening = false end)
            end
        end)
    end)
    return chip
end

function SL.RefreshTabs()
    for name, btn in pairs(SL.TabButtons) do
        local sel = (SL.ActiveTab == name)
        SL.Tween(btn, 0.2, {
            BackgroundColor3 = sel and SL.Theme.Accent or SL.Theme.Element,
            TextColor3 = sel and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or SL.Theme.SubText,
        })
    end
end

function SL.ApplySearch()
    local q = string.lower(SL.SearchBox.Text)
    local tab = SL.Tabs[SL.ActiveTab]
    if not tab then return end
    for _, item in ipairs(tab.Items) do
        if item.IsSection then
            item.Frame.Visible = (q == "")
        else
            item.Frame.Visible = (q == "") or (string.find(string.lower(item.Name), q, 1, true) ~= nil)
        end
    end
end
SL.SearchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(SL.ApplySearch)

function SL.SelectTab(name)
    SL.ActiveTab = name
    for n, t in pairs(SL.Tabs) do t.Page.Visible = (n == name) end
    SL.SearchBox.Text = ""
    SL.ApplySearch()
    SL.RefreshTabs()
end

function SL.CreateTab(name, icon)
    local btn = SL.New("TextButton", {
        BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Element, BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 28), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
        Text = (icon and (icon .. "  ") or "") .. name, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.SubText, TextSize = 11,
        LayoutOrder = #SL.Sidebar:GetChildren(),
    }, SL.Sidebar)
    SL.Round(btn, 6)
    SL.BindBg(btn, "BackgroundColor3", SL.THEMES.dark.Element, SL.THEMES.light.Element)
    SL.BindBg(btn, "TextColor3", SL.THEMES.dark.SubText, SL.THEMES.light.SubText)

    local page = SL.New("ScrollingFrame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = SL.Theme.Accent, Visible = false,
        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
    }, SL.Pages)
    SL.New("UIListLayout", {Padding = UDim.new(0, 5), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center}, page)
    SL.New("UIPadding", {PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 5), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4)}, page)

    local tab = {Page = page, Items = {}, Order = 0}
    SL.Tabs[name], SL.TabButtons[name] = tab, btn

    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if SL.ActiveTab ~= name then SL.Tween(btn, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.ElementHover}) end
    end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if SL.ActiveTab ~= name then SL.Tween(btn, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Element}) end
    end)
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() SL.SelectTab(name) end)

    local function nextOrder() tab.Order += 1 return tab.Order end
    local function register(frame, nm, isSection)
        table.insert(tab.Items, {Frame = frame, Name = nm, IsSection = isSection})
    end

    function tab:Section(text)
        local l = SL.New("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            Text = string.upper(text), TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Accent, TextSize = 10,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, LayoutOrder = nextOrder(),
        }, page)
        SL.BindAccent(l, "TextColor3")
        register(l, text, true)
        return l
    end

    function tab:Label(text)
        local l = SL.New("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
            Text = text, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.SubText, TextSize = 11, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            LayoutOrder = nextOrder(), TextWrapped = true,
        }, page)
        register(l, text)
        return l
    end

    function tab:Button(text, callback, nobind)
        local b = SL.New("TextButton", {
            BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Element, BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = text,
            TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text, TextSize = 12, LayoutOrder = nextOrder(),
        }, page)
        SL.Round(b, 6)
        b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() SL.Tween(b, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.ElementHover}) end)
        b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() SL.Tween(b, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Element}) end)
        local function run()
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok, err = pcall(callback)
                if not ok then
                    warn("[ScriptHub Error]: " .. text .. " -> " .. tostring(err))
                    SL.Notify("Ошибка", text .. ": " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 120), 5, true)
                end
            end)
        end
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            SL.Tween(b, 0.07, {BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Accent})
            task.delay(0.12, function() SL.Tween(b, 0.2, {BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Element}) end)
            run()
        end)
        if not nobind then
            SL.AttachBind(b, name .. "/" .. text, run, UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, 0))
        end
        register(b, text)
        return b
    end

    -- Кнопка, загружающая скрипт по ссылке
    function tab:Script(text, url)
        return tab:Button(text, function() SL.RunUrl(text, url) end)
    end

    function tab:Input(text, placeholder, callback)
        local f = SL.New("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Element, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), LayoutOrder = nextOrder()}, page)
        SL.Round(f, 6)
        SL.New("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.4, -12, 1, 0),
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = text, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text, TextSize = 12,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        }, f)
        local box = SL.New("TextBox", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 37), BorderSizePixel = 0,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.6, -12, 0, 22),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, PlaceholderText = placeholder or "", PlaceholderColor3 = SL.Theme.SubText,
            Text = "", TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text, TextSize = 11, ClearTextOnFocus = false, ClipsDescendants = true,
        }, f)
        SL.Round(box, 5)
        box.FocusLost:Connect(function() pcall(callback, box.Text) end)
        register(f, text)
        return box
    end

    function tab:CodeBox(placeholder, height)
        height = height or 150
        local f = SL.New("ScrollingFrame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 22), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height),
            LayoutOrder = nextOrder(), ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = SL.Theme.Accent,
            AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
            ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
        }, page)
        SL.Round(f, 6)
        local box = SL.New("TextBox", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 2), Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, height - 4),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, MultiLine = true, ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextEditable = true,
            Font = Enum.Font.Code, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 230),
            PlaceholderText = placeholder or "", PlaceholderColor3 = SL.Theme.SubText, Text = "",
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, TextWrapped = true,
        }, f)
        register(f, "Код")
        return box
    end

    function tab:Toggle(text, default, callback)
        local state = default and true or false
        local f = SL.New("TextButton", {
            BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Element, BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false, Text = "",
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), LayoutOrder = nextOrder(),
        }, page)
        SL.Round(f, 6)
        SL.New("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -124, 1, 0),
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = text, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text, TextSize = 12,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        }, f)
        local sw = SL.New("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 75), BorderSizePixel = 0,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 20),
        }, f)
        SL.Round(sw, 10)
        local knob = SL.New("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -8), Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16),
        }, sw)
        SL.Round(knob, 8)

        local obj = {}
        local function render(animate)
            local t = animate and 0.2 or 0
            SL.Tween(sw, t, {BackgroundColor3 = state and SL.Theme.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 75)})
            SL.Tween(knob, t, {Position = state and UDim2.new(1, -18, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -8)})
        end
        obj.Render = function() render(false) end

        function obj:Set(v, silent)
            state = v and true or false
            render(true)
            if not silent then
                task.spawn(function()
                    local ok, err = pcall(callback, state)
                    if not ok then warn("[ScriptHub Toggle Error]: " .. text .. " -> " .. tostring(err)) end
                end)
            end
        end
        function obj:Get() return state end

        f.MouseEnter:Connect(function() SL.Tween(f, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.ElementHover}) end)
        f.MouseLeave:Connect(function() SL.Tween(f, 0.15, {BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Element}) end)
        f.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() obj:Set(not state) end)

        SL.AttachBind(f, name .. "/" .. text, function()
            obj:Set(not state)
            SL.Notify(text, state and "ВКЛ" or "ВЫКЛ", 1.2)
        end, UDim2.new(1, -56, 0.5, 0))

        render(false)
        if state then task.spawn(function() pcall(callback, state) end) end
        table.insert(SL.Toggles, obj)
        register(f, text)
        return obj
    end

    function tab:Slider(text, min, max, default, callback, decimals)
        decimals = decimals or 0
        local value = math.clamp(default, min, max)
        local f = SL.New("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Element, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 46), LayoutOrder = nextOrder()}, page)
        SL.Round(f, 6)
        SL.New("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 4), Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 18),
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Text = text, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text, TextSize = 12,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        }, f)
        local valLabel = SL.New("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0, 4), Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 18),
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.SubText, TextSize = 11, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
        }, f)
        local track = SL.New("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 70), BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 31), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 6),
        }, f)
        SL.Round(track, 3)
        local fill = SL.New("Frame", {BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}, track)
        SL.BindAccent(fill, "BackgroundColor3")
        SL.Round(fill, 3)

        local function fmt(v)
            local m = 10 ^ decimals
            return math.floor(v * m + 0.5) / m
        end
        local obj = {}
        local function setValue(v, silent)
            value = fmt(math.clamp(v, min, max))
            local a = (value - min) / (max - min)
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(a, 0, 1, 0)
            valLabel.Text = tostring(value)
            if not silent then
                local ok, err = pcall(callback, value)
                if not ok then warn("[ScriptHub Slider Error]: " .. text .. " -> " .. tostring(err)) end
            end
        end
        function obj:Set(v) setValue(v, true) end
        function obj:Get() return value end

        local dragging = false
        local function fromInput(x)
            local a = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
            setValue(min + (max - min) * a)
        end
        track.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                page.ScrollingEnabled = false
                fromInput(i.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        SL.Add(SL.UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                fromInput(i.Position.X)
            end
        end))
        SL.Add(SL.UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                dragging = false
                page.ScrollingEnabled = true
                if obj.OnRelease then obj.OnRelease(value) end
            end
        end))

        setValue(value, true)
        register(f, text)
        return obj
    end

    return tab
end

-- ==================== ПРИМЕНЕНИЕ ТЕМЫ ====================

function SL.ApplyAccent(color, save)
    SL.Theme.Accent = color
    SL.Config.Accent = {math.floor(color.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.B * 255 + 0.5)}
    for i = #SL.AccentObjects, 1, -1 do
        local o = SL.AccentObjects[i]
        if o[1] and o[1].Parent then
            o[1][o[2]] = color
        else
            table.remove(SL.AccentObjects, i)
        end
    end
    for _, t in pairs(SL.Tabs) do t.Page.ScrollBarImageColor3 = color end
    for _, tg in ipairs(SL.Toggles) do tg.Render() end
    SL.RefreshTabs()
    if save then SL.SaveConfig() end
end

-- ==================== ОТКРЫТИЕ / ЗАКРЫТИЕ / СВОРАЧИВАНИЕ ====================
SL.minimized = false
SL.uiVisible = true
SL.ScreenshotMode = false

function SL.SetVisible(v)
    SL.uiVisible = v
    if v then
        SL.MainFrame.Visible = true
        SL.Tween(SL.MainFrame, 0.3, {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, SL.frameWidth, 0, SL.minimized and 38 or SL.frameHeight),
            BackgroundTransparency = SL.Config.Transparency,
        }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
    else
        SL.Tween(SL.MainFrame, 0.2, {Size = UDim2.new(0, SL.frameWidth, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
        task.delay(0.22, function() if not SL.uiVisible then SL.MainFrame.Visible = false end end)
    end
end

SL.MinBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    SL.minimized = not SL.minimized
    SL.MinBtn.Text = SL.minimized and "+" or "–"
    if SL.minimized then
        SL.Tween(SL.MainFrame, 0.25, {Size = UDim2.new(0, SL.frameWidth, 0, 38)})
        task.delay(0.26, function() if SL.minimized then SL.Body.Visible = false end end)
    else
        SL.Body.Visible = true
        SL.Tween(SL.MainFrame, 0.25, {Size = UDim2.new(0, SL.frameWidth, 0, SL.frameHeight)})
    end
end)

-- Плавающая кнопка для мобильных / быстрого открытия
SL.FloatBtn = SL.New("TextButton", {
    BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Top, Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -20), Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 40),
    Text = "☰", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 18, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text, AutoButtonColor = false,
    Visible = false, ZIndex = 60,
}, SL.ScreenGui)
SL.Round(SL.FloatBtn, 20)
SL.FloatStroke = SL.New("UIStroke", {Thickness = 2}, SL.FloatBtn)
SL.BindAccent(SL.FloatStroke, "Color")
SL.MakeDraggable(SL.FloatBtn, SL.FloatBtn)
SL.FloatBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() SL.SetVisible(not SL.uiVisible) end)
SL.CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() SL.SetVisible(false) SL.Notify("Меню скрыто", "Нажми " .. SL.Config.ToggleKey .. " или ☰, чтобы открыть", 3) end)
SL.FloatBtn.Visible = true

-- Горячая клавиша
SL.Add(SL.UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if SL.Listening then return end
    if gpe or SL.UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
    local kn = input.KeyCode.Name
    if kn == SL.Config.ToggleKey then
        if SL.ScreenshotMode then SL.ScreenshotMode = false SL.ScreenGui.Enabled = true else SL.SetVisible(not SL.uiVisible) end
        return
    end
    for id, key in pairs(SL.Config.Binds) do
        if key == kn and SL.Actions[id] then task.spawn(SL.Actions[id]) end
    end
end))


-- ==================== ПОЛНАЯ ВЫГРУЗКА ====================
SL.Unloading = false
function SL.UnloadHub()
    if SL.Unloading then return end
    SL.Unloading = true
    pcall(function()
        SL.Tween(SL.MainFrame, 0.25, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)})
    end)
    task.wait(0.27)
    for _, tg in ipairs(SL.Toggles) do pcall(function() if tg:Get() then tg:Set(false) end end) end
    for _, fn in ipairs(SL.Cleanups) do pcall(fn) end
    for _, c in ipairs(SL.Conns) do
        if typeof(c) == "RBXScriptConnection" then pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    end
    SL.Conns, SL.Cleanups = {}, {}
    pcall(function() SL.ScreenGui:Destroy() end)
    if getgenv then getgenv().HubUnload = nil end
    print("[Script Loader]: интерфейс выгружен!")
end
if getgenv then getgenv().HubUnload = SL.UnloadHub end

-- Мини-кнопка «выгрузить» в шапке
do
    local u = SL.TopButton("⏏", -94, Color3.fromRGB(120, 60, 60))
    u.MouseButton1Click:Connect(SL.UnloadHub)
end

function SL.CopyValue(label, value)
    if not setclipboard then error("Буфер обмена не поддерживается этим executor", 0) end
    setclipboard(tostring(value))
    SL.Notify("Скопировано", label, 1.8)
end

SL.ExtrasTab:Section("Локальные визуальные эффекты")
SL.extraEffects = {}
function SL.SetLocalEffect(name, className, props, enabled)
    local effect = SL.extraEffects[name]
    if enabled then
        if not effect or not effect.Parent then
            effect = Instance.new(className)
            effect.Name = "ScriptLoader_" .. name
            for k, v in pairs(props) do effect[k] = v end
            effect.Parent = SL.Lighting
            SL.extraEffects[name] = effect
        end
    elseif effect then
        effect:Destroy()
        SL.extraEffects[name] = nil
    end
end
SL.ExtrasTab:Toggle("15 · Мягкое размытие", false, function(v)
    SL.SetLocalEffect("Blur", "BlurEffect", {Size = 8}, v)
end)
SL.ExtrasTab:Toggle("16 · Цветовой фильтр", false, function(v)
    SL.SetLocalEffect("Color", "ColorCorrectionEffect", {Saturation = 0.15, Contrast = 0.08, TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(225, 235, 255)}, v)
end)
SL.ExtrasTab:Toggle("17 · Bloom", false, function(v)
    SL.SetLocalEffect("Bloom", "BloomEffect", {Intensity = 0.35, Size = 24, Threshold = 1.1}, v)
end)
SL.ExtrasTab:Toggle("18 · Sun Rays", false, function(v)
    SL.SetLocalEffect("SunRays", "SunRaysEffect", {Intensity = 0.08, Spread = 0.7}, v)
end)
SL.ExtrasTab:Button("19 · Удалить эффекты Script Loader", function()
    for name, effect in pairs(SL.extraEffects) do
        if effect and effect.Parent then effect:Destroy() end
        SL.extraEffects[name] = nil
    end
    SL.Notify("Эффекты", "Все дополнительные эффекты выключены", 2)
end, true)
SL.OnUnload(function()
    for _, effect in pairs(SL.extraEffects) do pcall(function() effect:Destroy() end) end
    table.clear(SL.extraEffects)
end)

-- ==================== FLING ====================
SL.FlingTab:Section("Скрипты")
SL.FlingTab:Script("Fling GUI", "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/K1LAS1K/Ultimate-Fling-GUI/main/flingscript.lua")
SL.FlingTab:Script("Invisible Fling", "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Gvobannyyyyy/88.5-invisible-fling-script/refs/heads/main/88.5%25%20accuracy%20invisible%20fling")
SL.FlingTab:Script("DropKick", "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/platinww/CrustyMain/refs/heads/main/universal/DropKick.lua")
SL.FlingTab:Script("Anti Fling", "https://scriptblox.com/raw/Universal-Script-Anti-fling-73205")
SL.FlingTab:Section("Встроенное")
SL.FlingTab:Button("Fling All", function()
    local function SkidFling(TargetPlayer)
        local Character = SL.LocalPlayer.Character
        local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local RootPart = Humanoid and Humanoid.RootPart
        local TCharacter = TargetPlayer.Character
        if not (TCharacter and Character and Humanoid and RootPart) then return end
        local THumanoid = TCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local TRootPart = THumanoid and THumanoid.RootPart
        local THead = TCharacter:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not (TRootPart or THead) then return end

        if RootPart.Velocity.Magnitude < 50 then getgenv().OldPos = RootPart.CFrame end

        local function FPos(BasePart, Pos, Ang)
            RootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(BasePart.Position) * Pos * Ang
            Character:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(BasePart.Position) * Pos * Ang)
            RootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(9e7, 9e7 * 10, 9e7)
            RootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(9e8, 9e8, 9e8)
        end
        local function SFBasePart(BasePart)
            local Time, Angle = tick(), 0
            repeat
                if RootPart and THumanoid then
                    Angle += 100
                    FPos(BasePart, CFrame.new(0, 1.5, 0) + THumanoid.MoveDirection * BasePart.Velocity.Magnitude / 1.25, CFrame.Angles(math.rad(Angle), 0, 0))
                    task.wait()
                else break end
            until BasePart.Velocity.Magnitude > 500 or BasePart.Parent ~= TargetPlayer.Character or Humanoid.Health <= 0 or tick() > Time + 2
        end

        local oldDestroyHeight = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
        workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0 / 0
        local BV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        BV.Velocity = Vector3.new(9e8, 9e8, 9e8)
        BV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1 / 0, 1 / 0, 1 / 0)
        BV.Parent = RootPart
        Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
        if TRootPart then SFBasePart(TRootPart) elseif THead then SFBasePart(THead) end
        BV:Destroy()
        Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, true)
        SL.Camera.CameraSubject = Humanoid
        -- Возврат на место (раньше персонаж оставался в точке финиша)
        pcall(function()
            RootPart.Velocity, RootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero, Vector3.zero
            if getgenv().OldPos then RootPart.CFrame = getgenv().OldPos end
        end)
        workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = oldDestroyHeight
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= SL.LocalPlayer then pcall(SkidFling, p) end
    end
end)

-- ==================== COMBAT ====================
SL.CombatTab:Section("Aimbot")
SL.aimbot = {Conns = {}, FOV = 150, Smooth = 0.35, TeamCheck = true, Circle = nil, Pred = 0}
SL.aiming = false
function SL.StopAimbot()
    for _, c in ipairs(SL.aimbot.Conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    SL.aimbot.Conns = {}
    SL.aiming = false
    if SL.aimbot.Circle then pcall(function() SL.aimbot.Circle:Remove() end) SL.aimbot.Circle = nil end
end
SL.OnUnload(SL.StopAimbot)

SL.CombatTab:Toggle("Aimbot (удерживать Q / ПКМ)", false, function(on)
    SL.StopAimbot()
    if not on then return end
    if Drawing then
        SL.aimbot.Circle = Drawing.new("Circle")
        SL.aimbot.Circle.Radius = SL.aimbot.FOV
        SL.aimbot.Circle.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
        SL.aimbot.Circle.Thickness = 1
        SL.aimbot.Circle.Transparency = 0.6
        SL.aimbot.Circle.Visible = true
    end
    local function getClosest()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local center = Vector2.new(cam.ViewportSize.X / 2, cam.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
        local closest, shortest = nil, math.huge
        for _, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= SL.LocalPlayer and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
                if not (SL.aimbot.TeamCheck and p.Team ~= nil and p.Team == SL.LocalPlayer.Team) then
                    local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                        local head = p.Character.Head
                        local sp, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position)
                        if onScreen then
                            local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Magnitude
                            if d <= SL.aimbot.FOV and d < shortest then shortest, closest = d, head end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return closest
    end
    table.insert(SL.aimbot.Conns, SL.UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i, gpe)
        if gpe then return end
        if i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then SL.aiming = true end
    end))
    table.insert(SL.aimbot.Conns, SL.UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
        if i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then SL.aiming = false end
    end))
    table.insert(SL.aimbot.Conns, SL.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if SL.aimbot.Circle then
            SL.aimbot.Circle.Position = Vector2.new(cam.ViewportSize.X / 2, cam.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
            SL.aimbot.Circle.Radius = SL.aimbot.FOV
        end
        if SL.aiming then
            local t = getClosest()
            if t then
                local goal = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, t.Position + t.AssemblyLinearVelocity * SL.aimbot.Pred)
                cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame:Lerp(goal, 1 - SL.aimbot.Smooth)
            end
        end
    end))
end)
SL.CombatTab:Slider("FOV радиус", 50, 500, 150, function(v) SL.aimbot.FOV = v end)
SL.CombatTab:Slider("Плавность наводки", 0, 0.95, 0.35, function(v) SL.aimbot.Smooth = v end, 2)
SL.CombatTab:Slider("Упреждение движения цели", 0, 0.4, 0, function(v) SL.aimbot.Pred = v end, 2)
SL.CombatTab:Toggle("Игнорировать свою команду", true, function(v) SL.aimbot.TeamCheck = v end)

SL.CombatTab:Section("Прочее")
SL.CombatTab:Script("Silent Aim", "https://scriptblox.com/raw/Universal-Script-silent-aim-UNIVERSAL-65225")

SL.trigConn = nil
SL.CombatTab:Toggle("Triggerbot", false, function(on)
    if SL.trigConn then SL.trigConn:Disconnect() SL.trigConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    local mouse = SL.LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
    local busy = false
    SL.trigConn = SL.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if busy then return end
        local tgt = mouse.Target
        if tgt and tgt.Parent then
            local hum = tgt.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or (tgt.Parent.Parent and tgt.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"))
            if hum and hum.Health > 0 and hum.Parent ~= SL.LocalPlayer.Character then
                busy = true
                SL.ClickOnce()
                task.wait(0.05)
                busy = false
            end
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.trigConn then SL.trigConn:Disconnect() end end)

SL.spinSpeed, SL.spinConn = 20, nil

    if SL.spinConn then SL.spinConn:Disconnect() SL.spinConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    SL.spinConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local root = SL.GetRoot()
        if root then root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(SL.spinSpeed), 0) end
    end)
end)

SL.CombatTab:Section("Ближний бой")

SL.ka = {on = false, range = 15, rate = 0.1, face = true, team = true}
SL.CombatTab:Toggle("Kill Aura (авто-удар инструментом)", false, function(on)
    SL.ka.on = on
    if not on then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while SL.ka.on do
            local c, root = SL.GetChar(), SL.GetRoot()
            local tool = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            if tool and root then
                local target = SL.nearestEnemy(SL.ka.range, SL.ka.team)
                if target then
                    local tr = target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if tr then
                        if SL.ka.face then
                            root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(root.Position, Vector3.new(tr.Position.X, root.Position.Y, tr.Position.Z))
                        end
                        tool:Activate()
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(SL.ka.rate)
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() SL.ka.on = false end)
SL.CombatTab:Slider("Радиус Kill Aura", 5, 60, 15, function(v) SL.ka.range = v end)
SL.CombatTab:Slider("Задержка между ударами (сек)", 0.03, 1, 0.1, function(v) SL.ka.rate = v end, 2)
SL.CombatTab:Toggle("Kill Aura: поворачиваться к цели", true, function(v) SL.ka.face = v end)
SL.CombatTab:Toggle("Kill Aura: игнорировать свою команду", true, function(v) SL.ka.team = v end)

SL.reach = {on = false, size = 12, saved = {}}
function SL.RestoreReach()
    for handle, d in pairs(SL.reach.saved) do
        if handle and handle.Parent then handle.Size, handle.Massless, handle.CanCollide = d.Size, d.Massless, d.CanCollide end
    end
    SL.reach.saved = {}
end
SL.CombatTab:Toggle("Reach (увеличить хитбокс оружия)", false, function(on)
    SL.reach.on = on
    if not on then SL.RestoreReach() return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while SL.reach.on do
            local c = SL.GetChar()
            local tool = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            local handle = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
            if handle then
                if not SL.reach.saved[handle] then
                    SL.reach.saved[handle] = {Size = handle.Size, Massless = handle.Massless, CanCollide = handle.CanCollide}
                end
                handle.Size = Vector3.new(SL.reach.size, SL.reach.size, SL.reach.size)
                handle.Massless = true
                handle.CanCollide = false
            end
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() SL.reach.on = false SL.RestoreReach() end)
SL.CombatTab:Slider("Размер Reach", 3, 40, 12, function(v) SL.reach.size = v end)

SL.CombatTab:Section("Защита")
SL.akConn = nil
SL.CombatTab:Toggle("Анти-отбрасывание", false, function(on)
    if SL.akConn then SL.akConn:Disconnect() SL.akConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    SL.akConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local h, r = SL.GetHum(), SL.GetRoot()
        if not (h and r) or h.PlatformStand then return end
        local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local horiz = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
        if horiz.Magnitude > math.max(100, h.WalkSpeed * 3) then
            r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, v.Y, 0)
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.akConn then SL.akConn:Disconnect() end end)

    SL.thud.on = false
    if SL.thud.frame then SL.thud.frame:Destroy() SL.thud.frame = nil end
end
SL.CombatTab:Toggle("Target HUD (ближайший игрок)", false, function(on)
    SL.StopTHUD()
    if not on then return end
    SL.thud.on = true
    local f = SL.New("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Top, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 70),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 210, 0, 46), ZIndex = 30,
    }, SL.ScreenGui)
    SL.Round(f, 8)
    SL.New("UIStroke", {Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 80), Thickness = 1}, f)
    local nameL = SL.New("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 4), Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 16),
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text, Text = "—",
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 31,
    }, f)
    local barBg = SL.New("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 58), BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 28), Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 8), ZIndex = 31,
    }, f)
    SL.Round(barBg, 4)
    local fill = SL.New("Frame", {BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), ZIndex = 32}, barBg)
    SL.Round(fill, 4)
    SL.thud.frame = f
    task.spawn(function()
        while SL.thud.on and SL.thud.frame do
            local target, dist, hum = SL.nearestEnemy(math.huge, false)
            if target and hum then
                local ratio = math.clamp(hum.Health / math.max(hum.MaxHealth, 1), 0, 1)
                nameL.Text = ("%s | %d HP | %dm"):format(target.DisplayName, math.floor(hum.Health), math.floor(dist))
                SL.Tween(fill, 0.1, {Size = UDim2.new(ratio, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(0.33 * ratio, 0.8, 0.9)})
            else
                nameL.Text = "Нет целей"
                fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(SL.StopTHUD)

SL.CombatTab:Button("Прыгнуть за спину к ближайшему", function()

    SL.Notify("Combat", "Прыжок за спину", 1.5)
end)

SL.CombatTab:Section("Урон и ХП цели")
SL.CombatTab:Section("Визуальные бои")
SL.showDmgConn, SL.dmgConns = nil, {}
SL.CombatTab:Toggle("Показывать урон над головами", false, function(on)
    for _, c in ipairs(SL.dmgConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    SL.dmgConns = {}
    if not on then return end
    for _, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local function hookHP(char)
            local h = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not h then return end
            local last = h.Health
            local c = h:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
                local cur = h.Health
                local diff = last - cur
                last = cur
                if diff <= 0 then return end
                local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if not head then return end
                local bb = SL.New("BillboardGui", {Size = UDim2.new(0,60,0,24), StudsOffset = Vector3.new(math.random(-2,2),4+math.random(0,3),0), AlwaysOnTop = true}, head)
                local lbl = SL.New("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0), Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack, TextSize=13, TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,60,60), Text="-"..math.floor(diff), TextStrokeTransparency=0.3}, bb)
                task.delay(0.8, function() SL.Tween(lbl,0.4,{TextTransparency=1}) task.wait(0.4) bb:Destroy() end)
            end)
            table.insert(SL.dmgConns, c)
        end
        local function hookPlayer(pl)
            if pl.Character then hookHP(pl.Character) end
            table.insert(SL.dmgConns, pl.CharacterAdded:Connect(hookHP))
        end
        hookPlayer(p)
    end
    table.insert(SL.dmgConns, SL.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(pl)
        pl.CharacterAdded:Connect(hookHP)
    end))
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() for _, c in ipairs(SL.dmgConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end end)

SL.pingBot = {on=false, dist=8, conn=nil, timer=0}
SL.CombatTab:Toggle("Ping Abuse TP (мелкий TP)", false, function(on)
    if SL.pingBot.conn then SL.pingBot.conn:Disconnect() SL.pingBot.conn = nil end
    SL.pingBot.on = on
    if not on then return end
    SL.pingBot.conn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        local r = SL.GetRoot()
        if not r then return end
        SL.pingBot.timer += dt
        if SL.pingBot.timer < 0.07 then return end
        SL.pingBot.timer = 0
        local d = SL.pingBot.dist
        local offs = {Vector3.new(d,0,0),Vector3.new(-d,0,0),Vector3.new(0,0,d),Vector3.new(0,0,-d)}
        local o = offs[math.random(#offs)]
        local saved = r.Position
        r.CFrame = CFrame.new(saved + o)
        task.defer(function() if r and r.Parent then r.CFrame = CFrame.new(saved) end end)
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.pingBot.conn then SL.pingBot.conn:Disconnect() end end)
SL.CombatTab:Slider("Ping Abuse дистанция", 2, 20, 8, function(v) SL.pingBot.dist = v end)

-- ==================== RAGE ====================
function SL.camYaw()
    local look = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
    return math.atan2(-look.X, -look.Z)
end

SL.RageTab:Section("Bunnyhop")
-- В Roblox зажатый Space и так прыгает без остановки, поэтому главное здесь — разгон скорости.
SL.bh = {progressive = true, step = 4, limit = 70, fixed = 32, base = 16, grace = 0.15,
    strafe = true, forceJump = false, cur = 16, conn = nil}
SL.bhLabel = SL.RageTab:Label("Скорость: 16 / 70")
function SL.bhRefreshLabel()
    SL.bhLabel.Text = ("Скорость: %d / %d"):format(math.floor(SL.bh.cur + 0.5), SL.bh.progressive and SL.bh.limit or SL.bh.fixed)
end
SL.RageTab:Toggle("Bunnyhop (держи Space)", false, function(on)
    if SL.bh.conn then SL.bh.conn:Disconnect() SL.bh.conn = nil end
    SL.bh.cur = SL.bh.base
    SL.bhRefreshLabel()
    if not on then return end
    local wasGrounded, groundTimer, uiTimer = true, 0, 0
    SL.bh.conn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        local h, r = SL.GetHum(), SL.GetRoot()
        if not (h and r) or h.Health <= 0 then return end
        local holding = (SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) and not SL.UIS:GetFocusedTextBox()) or h.Jump
        local grounded = h.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air
        local md = h.MoveDirection

        if grounded then
            groundTimer += dt
            if SL.bh.forceJump and holding then h:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
            -- не успел прыгнуть вовремя или отпустил Space — скорость сбрасывается
            if groundTimer > SL.bh.grace or not holding then SL.bh.cur = SL.bh.base end
        elseif wasGrounded then
            -- момент отрыва от земли = новый прыжок
            groundTimer = 0
            if holding then
                if SL.bh.progressive then
                    SL.bh.cur = math.min(SL.bh.limit, math.max(SL.bh.cur, SL.bh.base) + SL.bh.step)
                else
                    SL.bh.cur = SL.bh.fixed
                end
            else
                SL.bh.cur = SL.bh.base -- просто сошёл с уступа
            end
        end
        wasGrounded = grounded

        if md.Magnitude > 0 and SL.bh.cur > SL.bh.base and (not grounded or groundTimer <= SL.bh.grace) then
            local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            local horiz = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
            local target = md.Unit * SL.bh.cur
            local nv = grounded and target or (SL.bh.strafe and horiz:Lerp(target, 0.2) or horiz)
            r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(nv.X, v.Y, nv.Z)
        end

        uiTimer += dt
        if uiTimer > 0.1 then uiTimer = 0 SL.bhRefreshLabel() end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.bh.conn then SL.bh.conn:Disconnect() end end)
SL.RageTab:Toggle("Прогрессивная скорость (как в CS 2)", true, function(v) SL.bh.progressive = v end)
SL.RageTab:Slider("Прирост за прыжок", 1, 12, 4, function(v) SL.bh.step = v end)
SL.RageTab:Slider("Лимит скорости (прогрессивная)", 30, 120, 70, function(v) SL.bh.limit = v end)
SL.RageTab:Slider("Фикс. скорость (без прогрессии)", 16, 120, 32, function(v) SL.bh.fixed = v end)
SL.RageTab:Slider("Окно между прыжками (сек)", 0.05, 0.5, 0.15, function(v) SL.bh.grace = v end, 2)
SL.RageTab:Toggle("Контроль в воздухе (auto-strafe)", true, function(v) SL.bh.strafe = v end)
SL.RageTab:Toggle("Принудительный авто-прыжок", false, function(v) SL.bh.forceJump = v end)

SL.RageTab:Section("Anti-Aim")
SL.aa = {mode = 1, speed = 30, jitter = 60, conn = nil}
SL.aaModes = {"Backward", "Spin", "Jitter", "Sideways", "Random"}
SL.aaBtn = nil
SL.aaBtn = SL.RageTab:Button("Режим: Backward (нажми для смены)", function()
    SL.aa.mode = SL.aa.mode % #SL.aaModes + 1
    SL.aaBtn.Text = "Режим: " .. SL.aaModes[SL.aa.mode] .. " (нажми для смены)"
end, true)
function SL.StopAA()
    if SL.aa.conn then SL.aa.conn:Disconnect() SL.aa.conn = nil end
    local h = SL.GetHum()
    if h then h.AutoRotate = true end
end
SL.RageTab:Toggle("Anti-Aim", false, function(on)
    SL.StopAA()
    if not on then return end
    local spinAngle, flip = 0, 1
    SL.aa.conn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local h, r = SL.GetHum(), SL.GetRoot()
        if not (h and r) or h.Health <= 0 then return end
        h.AutoRotate = false
        local base, mode, yaw = SL.camYaw(), SL.aaModes[SL.aa.mode], 0
        if mode == "Backward" then
            yaw = base + math.pi
        elseif mode == "Spin" then
            spinAngle += math.rad(SL.aa.speed)
            yaw = spinAngle
        elseif mode == "Jitter" then
            flip = -flip
            yaw = base + math.pi + math.rad(SL.aa.jitter) * flip
        elseif mode == "Sideways" then
            yaw = base + math.pi / 2
        else
            yaw = math.random() * math.pi * 2
        end
        r.CFrame = CFrame.new(r.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(SL.StopAA)
SL.RageTab:Slider("Скорость Spin", 5, 90, 30, function(v) SL.aa.speed = v end)
SL.RageTab:Slider("Угол Jitter", 10, 180, 60, function(v) SL.aa.jitter = v end)

SL.fl = {on = false, interval = 0.4, duration = 0.15}
function SL.StopFL()

    local r = SL.GetRoot()

end

            local r = SL.GetRoot()
            if r then
                r.Anchored = true

            end

        end
    end)
end)

SL.RageTab:Section("Rage Aimbot")
SL.rage = {part = 1, wall = false, auto = false, team = true, conn = nil, last = 0}
SL.ragePartNames = {"Head", "HumanoidRootPart"}
SL.ragePartLabels = {"Голова", "Торс"}
function SL.visibleCheck(part)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {SL.GetChar(), cam}
    local origin = cam.CFrame.Position
    local res = workspace:Raycast(origin, part.Position - origin, params)
    return res == nil or res.Instance:IsDescendantOf(part.Parent)
end
function SL.rageTarget()
    local root = SL.GetRoot()
    if not root then return nil end
    local best, bd = nil, math.huge
    for _, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= SL.LocalPlayer and p.Character and not (SL.rage.team and p.Team ~= nil and p.Team == SL.LocalPlayer.Team) then
            local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local part = p.Character:FindFirstChild(SL.ragePartNames[SL.rage.part]) or p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hum and hum.Health > 0 and part then
                local d = (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if d < bd and (not SL.rage.wall or SL.visibleCheck(part)) then bd, best = d, part end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end
SL.RageTab:Toggle("Rage Aimbot (без FOV, мгновенный)", false, function(on)
    if SL.rage.conn then SL.rage.conn:Disconnect() SL.rage.conn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    SL.rage.conn = SL.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local t = SL.rageTarget()
        if not t then return end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, t.Position)
        if SL.rage.auto and tick() - SL.rage.last > 0.08 then
            SL.rage.last = tick()
            task.spawn(SL.ClickOnce)
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.rage.conn then SL.rage.conn:Disconnect() end end)
SL.partBtn = nil
SL.partBtn = SL.RageTab:Button("Цель: Голова (нажми для смены)", function()
    SL.rage.part = SL.rage.part % #SL.ragePartNames + 1
    SL.partBtn.Text = "Цель: " .. SL.ragePartLabels[SL.rage.part] .. " (нажми для смены)"
end, true)
SL.RageTab:Toggle("Авто-стрельба", false, function(v) SL.rage.auto = v end)
SL.RageTab:Toggle("Только видимые цели (wall check)", false, function(v) SL.rage.wall = v end)
SL.RageTab:Toggle("Игнорировать свою команду", true, function(v) SL.rage.team = v end)

SL.RageTab:Section("Hitbox Expander")
SL.hb = {size = 10, orig = {}, conn = nil}
function SL.RestoreHitboxes()
    for part, d in pairs(SL.hb.orig) do
        if part and part.Parent then
            part.Size, part.Transparency, part.CanCollide = d.Size, d.Transparency, d.CanCollide
        end
    end
    SL.hb.orig = {}
end
SL.RageTab:Toggle("Hitbox Expander", false, function(on)
    if SL.hb.conn then SL.hb.conn:Disconnect() SL.hb.conn = nil end
    SL.RestoreHitboxes()
    if not on then return end
    SL.hb.conn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        for _, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do
            local root = p ~= SL.LocalPlayer and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if root then
                if not SL.hb.orig[root] then
                    SL.hb.orig[root] = {Size = root.Size, Transparency = root.Transparency, CanCollide = root.CanCollide}
                end
                root.Size = Vector3.new(SL.hb.size, SL.hb.size, SL.hb.size)
                root.Transparency = 0.7
                root.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.hb.conn then SL.hb.conn:Disconnect() end SL.RestoreHitboxes() end)
SL.RageTab:Slider("Размер хитбокса", 2, 30, 10, function(v) SL.hb.size = v end)


SL.RageTab:Section("Velocity Abuse")
SL.velAbuse = {on=false, force=200, dir="Up", conn=nil}
SL.velDirs = {"Up","Forward","Random","Backward"}
SL.velDirBtn = nil
SL.velDirBtn = SL.RageTab:Button("Направление: Up (нажми для смены)", function()
    local i = 1
    for j, v in ipairs(SL.velDirs) do if v == SL.velAbuse.dir then i = j break end end
    SL.velAbuse.dir = SL.velDirs[i % #SL.velDirs + 1]
    SL.velDirBtn.Text = "Направление: " .. SL.velAbuse.dir .. " (нажми для смены)"
end, true)
function SL.doVelAbuse()
    local r, cam = SL.GetRoot(), workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not r then return end
    local dir
    if SL.velAbuse.dir == "Up" then dir = Vector3.new(0,1,0)
    elseif SL.velAbuse.dir == "Forward" then dir = cam.CFrame.LookVector
    elseif SL.velAbuse.dir == "Backward" then dir = -cam.CFrame.LookVector
    else dir = Vector3.new(math.random()-0.5, math.random()-0.5, math.random()-0.5).Unit end
    r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = dir * SL.velAbuse.force
end
SL.RageTab:Toggle("Velocity Abuse (V / кнопка ниже)", false, function(on)
    if SL.velAbuse.conn then SL.velAbuse.conn:Disconnect() SL.velAbuse.conn = nil end
    SL.velAbuse.on = on
    if not on then return end
    SL.velAbuse.conn = SL.UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i, gpe)
        if gpe then return end
        if i.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.V then SL.doVelAbuse() end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.velAbuse.conn then SL.velAbuse.conn:Disconnect() end end)
SL.RageTab:Button("💥 Velocity Abuse (тап / клик)", function() SL.doVelAbuse() end)
SL.RageTab:Slider("Сила броска", 50, 1000, 200, function(v) SL.velAbuse.force = v end)

SL.RageTab:Section("Orbit")
SL.orbit = {on=false, conn=nil, radius=8, speed=2, target=nil, angle=0}
SL.RageTab:Toggle("Orbit вокруг ближайшего", false, function(on)
    if SL.orbit.conn then SL.orbit.conn:Disconnect() SL.orbit.conn = nil end
    SL.orbit.on = on
    if not on then
        local r = SL.GetRoot()
        if r then r.Anchored = false end
        return
    end
    SL.orbit.target = SL.nearestEnemy(math.huge, false)
    if not SL.orbit.target then
        SL.Notify("Orbit", "Цель не найдена", 2, true)
        return
    end
    local r = SL.GetRoot()
    if r then r.Anchored = true end
    SL.orbit.conn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        local tr = SL.orbit.target and SL.orbit.target.Character and SL.orbit.target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local myR = SL.GetRoot()
        if not (tr and myR) then return end
        SL.orbit.angle += dt * SL.orbit.speed
        local x = tr.Position.X + math.cos(SL.orbit.angle) * SL.orbit.radius
        local z = tr.Position.Z + math.sin(SL.orbit.angle) * SL.orbit.radius
        myR.CFrame = CFrame.new(x, tr.Position.Y + 2, z)
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function()
    if SL.orbit.conn then SL.orbit.conn:Disconnect() end
    local r = SL.GetRoot() if r then r.Anchored = false end
end)

SL.RageTab:Slider("Скорость орбиты", 0.5, 10, 2, function(v) SL.orbit.speed = v end)

SL.RageTab:Section("Aimbot Prediction+")
SL.predShoot = {on=false, conn=nil, delay=0.08}
SL.RageTab:Toggle("Predicted Shot (задержка + упреждение)", false, function(on)
    if SL.predShoot.conn then SL.predShoot.conn:Disconnect() SL.predShoot.conn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    SL.predShoot.conn = SL.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q) and not SL.UIS:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) then return end
        local target, _, _ = SL.nearestEnemy(math.huge, SL.aimbot and SL.aimbot.TeamCheck or true)
        if not target then return end
        local part = target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not part then return end
        local vel = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local predicted = part.Position + vel * SL.predShoot.delay
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, predicted)
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.predShoot.conn then SL.predShoot.conn:Disconnect() end end)
SL.RageTab:Slider("Задержка предсказания (сек)", 0.02, 0.3, 0.08, function(v) SL.predShoot.delay = v end, 2)

SL.MoveTab:Section("Полёт и прочее")
SL.flySpeed, SL.flyConn, SL.flyBV, SL.flyBG = 60, nil, nil, nil
function SL.StopFly()
    if SL.flyConn then SL.flyConn:Disconnect() SL.flyConn = nil end
    if SL.flyBV then SL.flyBV:Destroy() SL.flyBV = nil end
    if SL.flyBG then SL.flyBG:Destroy() SL.flyBG = nil end
    local h, r = SL.GetHum(), SL.GetRoot()
    if h then h.PlatformStand = false end
    if r then r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end
end
SL.MoveTab:Toggle("Fly (WASD + Space / Shift)", false, function(on)
    SL.StopFly()
    if not on then return end
    SL.flyConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local h, r = SL.GetHum(), SL.GetRoot()
        if not (h and r) then return end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera

        -- BodyVelocity держит скорость (и гасит гравитацию), BodyGyro разворачивает персонажа за камерой
        if not SL.flyBV or SL.flyBV.Parent ~= r then
            if SL.flyBV then SL.flyBV:Destroy() end
            SL.flyBV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
            SL.flyBV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
            SL.flyBV.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            SL.flyBV.Parent = r
        end
        if not SL.flyBG or SL.flyBG.Parent ~= r then
            if SL.flyBG then SL.flyBG:Destroy() end
            SL.flyBG = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
            SL.flyBG.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
            SL.flyBG.P = 9e4
            SL.flyBG.D = 1000
            SL.flyBG.Parent = r
        end
        h.PlatformStand = true

        local dir = Vector3.zero
        if SL.UIS.TouchEnabled and not SL.UIS.KeyboardEnabled then
            dir = h.MoveDirection
            if h.Jump then dir += Vector3.yAxis end
        else
            if SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then dir += cam.CFrame.LookVector end
            if SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then dir -= cam.CFrame.LookVector end
            if SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then dir += cam.CFrame.RightVector end
            if SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then dir -= cam.CFrame.RightVector end
            if SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then dir += Vector3.yAxis end
            if SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then dir -= Vector3.yAxis end
        end
        SL.flyBV.Velocity = dir.Magnitude > 0 and dir.Unit * SL.flySpeed or Vector3.zero
        SL.flyBG.CFrame = cam.CFrame
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(SL.StopFly)
SL.MoveTab:Slider("Скорость полёта", 10, 300, 60, function(v) SL.flySpeed = v end)

SL.origGravity = workspace.Gravity
SL.MoveTab:Slider("Гравитация", 0, 400, math.floor(SL.origGravity), function(v) workspace.Gravity = v end)
SL.OnUnload(function() workspace.Gravity = SL.origGravity end)

    if SL.ctpConn then SL.ctpConn:Disconnect() SL.ctpConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    SL.ctpConn = SL.UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i, gpe)
        if gpe then return end
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then
            local r, m = SL.GetRoot(), SL.LocalPlayer:GetMouse()

        end
    end)
end)

SL.MoveTab:Section("Следование за игроком")
SL.followName, SL.followDist, SL.followConn, SL.followTg = "", 4, nil, nil
function SL.findPlayer(q)
    q = string.lower(q or "")
    if q == "" then return nil end
    local best
    for _, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= SL.LocalPlayer then
            local n, d = string.lower(p.Name), string.lower(p.DisplayName)
            if n == q or d == q then return p end
            if not best and (string.sub(n, 1, #q) == q or string.sub(d, 1, #q) == q) then best = p end
        end
    end
    return best
end
SL.MoveTab:Input("Ник игрока", "часть ника...", function(t) SL.followName = t end)
SL.MoveTab:Slider("Дистанция", 2, 20, 4, function(v) SL.followDist = v end)
SL.followTg = SL.MoveTab:Toggle("Следовать за игроком", false, function(on)
    if SL.followConn then SL.followConn:Disconnect() SL.followConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    local target = SL.findPlayer(SL.followName)
    if not target then
        SL.Notify("Follow", "Игрок не найден", 3, true)
        task.defer(function() if SL.followTg then SL.followTg:Set(false, true) end end)
        return
    end
    SL.Notify("Follow", "Следую за " .. target.Name, 2)
    SL.followConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not target.Parent then
            SL.followConn:Disconnect() SL.followConn = nil
            if SL.followTg then SL.followTg:Set(false, true) end
            return
        end
        local h, r = SL.GetHum(), SL.GetRoot()
        local tr = target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not (h and r and tr) then return end
        if (r.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude > SL.followDist + 1 then
            h:MoveTo(tr.Position)
            if tr.Position.Y - r.Position.Y > 3 then h.Jump = true end
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.followConn then SL.followConn:Disconnect() end end)
SL.MoveTab:Button("Телепорт к игроку", function()
    local target = SL.findPlayer(SL.followName)
    local r = SL.GetRoot()
    local tr = target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if r and tr then r.CFrame = tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3) else error("игрок не найден", 0) end
end)

SL.speedHack = {on=false, conn=nil, mult=1.5}
SL.normalSpeed = 16
SL.MoveTab:Toggle("Speed Hack (множитель скорости)", false, function(on)
    if SL.speedHack.conn then SL.speedHack.conn:Disconnect() SL.speedHack.conn = nil end
    SL.speedHack.on = on

        local h = SL.GetHum()
        if h then h.WalkSpeed = SL.normalSpeed end
        return
    end
    SL.speedHack.conn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local h = SL.GetHum()
        if not h then return end
        if type(SL.speedOn) == "boolean" and SL.speedOn then return end
        SL.normalSpeed = 16
        h.WalkSpeed = SL.normalSpeed * SL.speedHack.mult
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function()
    if SL.speedHack.conn then SL.speedHack.conn:Disconnect() end
    local h = SL.GetHum() if h then h.WalkSpeed = 16 end
end)

SL.MoveTab:Toggle("Swim Fly (летать в воде/воздухе)", false, function(on)
    if SL.swimConn then SL.swimConn:Disconnect() SL.swimConn = nil end
    if not on then
        local h = SL.GetHum()
        if h then h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming, true) end
        return
    end
    SL.swimConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local h, r = SL.GetHum(), SL.GetRoot()
        if not (h and r) then return end
        if h:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then
            r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 15, r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
        end
    end)
end)

SL.longJumpConn = nil
SL.MoveTab:Toggle("Long Jump (ускорение при прыжке)", false, function(on)
    if SL.longJumpConn then SL.longJumpConn:Disconnect() SL.longJumpConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    local lastY = 0
    SL.longJumpConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        local r, h = SL.GetRoot(), SL.GetHum()
        if not (r and h) then return end
        local curY = r.Position.Y
        if h.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air and curY > lastY then
            local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X * 1.03, v.Y, v.Z * 1.03)
        end
        lastY = curY
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.longJumpConn then SL.longJumpConn:Disconnect() end end)

    if not on then return end

        if not (h and r) then return end

            local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity

SL.jetConn, SL.jetActive = nil, false
SL.MoveTab:Toggle("Jetpack (держи Space в воздухе)", false, function(on)
    if SL.jetConn then SL.jetConn:Disconnect() SL.jetConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    SL.jetConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        local h, r = SL.GetHum(), SL.GetRoot()
        if not (h and r) then return end
        local inAir = h.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
        local jumping = SL.isMobile
            and (h:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping or h:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FreeFalling)
            or (SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) and not SL.UIS:GetFocusedTextBox())
        if inAir and jumping then
            local v = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, math.min(v.Y + 60*dt, 50), v.Z)
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.jetConn then SL.jetConn:Disconnect() end end)

SL.MoveTab:Button("📍 Поставить маркер", function()
