    if not r then error("персонаж не найден", 0) end
    SL.markers[SL.markerName] = r.CFrame
    SL.Notify("Маркер", "'" .. SL.markerName .. "' сохранён", 2)
end)
SL.MoveTab:Button("↩ TP к маркеру", function()
    local r = SL.GetRoot()
    if not (r and SL.markers[SL.markerName]) then error("маркер '" .. SL.markerName .. "' не найден", 0) end
    r.CFrame = SL.markers[SL.markerName]
end)

    local names = {}
    for k in pairs(SL.markers) do table.insert(names, k) end
    SL.Notify("Маркеры", #names > 0 and table.concat(names, ", ") or "пусто", 4, true)
end)

SL.warpConn, SL.warpTarget = nil, Vector3.new(0, 50, 0)
SL.MoveTab:Toggle("Warp Loop (мигание на точку и назад)", false, function(on)
    if SL.warpConn then SL.warpConn:Disconnect() SL.warpConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    local r = SL.GetRoot()
    if r then SL.warpTarget = r.Position + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0) end
    local frame, interval = 0, 0
    SL.warpConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        local myR = SL.GetRoot()
        if not myR then return end
        interval += dt
        if interval < 0.13 then return end
        interval = 0
        local saved = myR.Position
        myR.CFrame = CFrame.new(SL.warpTarget)
        task.defer(function()
            if myR and myR.Parent then myR.CFrame = CFrame.new(saved) end
        end)
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.warpConn then SL.warpConn:Disconnect() end end)

    if on then
        SL.fbSaved = {SL.Lighting.Brightness, SL.Lighting.FogEnd, SL.Lighting.OutdoorAmbient}
        SL.Lighting.Brightness = 2
        SL.Lighting.FogEnd = 1e6
        SL.Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
    elseif SL.fbSaved then
        SL.Lighting.Brightness, SL.Lighting.FogEnd, SL.Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = SL.fbSaved[1], SL.fbSaved[2], SL.fbSaved[3]
        SL.fbSaved = nil
    end

end

SL.rainConn, SL.rainFolder, SL.rainSound, SL.rainSaved = nil, nil, nil, nil
SL.VisualsTab:Toggle("Rain Atmosphere", false, function(on)
    if SL.rainConn then SL.rainConn:Disconnect() SL.rainConn = nil end
    if SL.rainFolder then SL.rainFolder:Destroy() SL.rainFolder = nil end
    if SL.rainSound then SL.rainSound:Destroy() SL.rainSound = nil end
    if not on then
        if SL.rainSaved then
            SL.Lighting.Brightness, SL.Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = SL.rainSaved[1], SL.rainSaved[2]
            SL.rainSaved = nil
        end
        return
    end
    SL.rainSaved = {SL.Lighting.Brightness, SL.Lighting.OutdoorAmbient}
    SL.Lighting.Brightness = 0.2
    SL.Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 60)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    SL.rainSound = SL.New("Sound", {SoundId = "rbxassetid://9064263922", Volume = 0.5, Looped = true}, cam)
    SL.rainSound:Play()
    SL.rainFolder = SL.New("Folder", {Name = "HubRain"}, cam)
    SL.rainConn = SL.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local root = SL.GetRoot()
        if not root then return end
        for _ = 1, 6 do
            local drop = SL.New("Part", {
                Size = Vector3.new(0.06, 7, 0.06), Color = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 200), Transparency = 0.7,
                Material = Enum.Material.Neon, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
                Anchored = false,
            })
            drop.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position + Vector3.new(math.random(-125, 125), 60, math.random(-125, 125)))
            drop.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -120, 0)
            drop.Parent = SL.rainFolder
            task.delay(1.1, function() drop:Destroy() end)
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function()
    if SL.rainConn then SL.rainConn:Disconnect() end
    if SL.rainFolder then SL.rainFolder:Destroy() end
    if SL.rainSound then SL.rainSound:Destroy() end
end)

SL.VisualsTab:Section("ESP (встроенный)")
SL.esp = {on = false, objs = {}}
function SL.espClear()
    for p, e in pairs(SL.esp.objs) do
        pcall(function() e.hl:Destroy() e.bb:Destroy() end)
        SL.esp.objs[p] = nil
    end
end
SL.VisualsTab:Toggle("ESP: подсветка + ник + дистанция", false, function(on)
    SL.esp.on = on
    if not on then SL.espClear() return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while SL.esp.on do
            local myRoot = SL.GetRoot()
            for _, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= SL.LocalPlayer then
                    local c = p.Character
                    local head = c and c:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local e = SL.esp.objs[p]
                    if c and head and root then
                        if not e or e.hl.Parent ~= c then
                            if e then pcall(function() e.hl:Destroy() e.bb:Destroy() end) end
                            local col = (p.Team and p.TeamColor.Color) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
                            local hl = SL.New("Highlight", {
                                FillColor = col, OutlineColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), FillTransparency = 0.6,
                                DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop,
                            }, c)
                            local bb = SL.New("BillboardGui", {
                                Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 28), StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), AlwaysOnTop = true,
                            }, head)
                            local lbl = SL.New("TextLabel", {
                                BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                                TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4,
                            }, bb)
                            e = {hl = hl, bb = bb, lbl = lbl}
                            SL.esp.objs[p] = e
                        end
                        local d = myRoot and math.floor((myRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude) or 0
                        e.lbl.Text = p.DisplayName .. " [" .. d .. "m]"
                    elseif e then
                        pcall(function() e.hl:Destroy() e.bb:Destroy() end)
                        SL.esp.objs[p] = nil
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() SL.esp.on = false SL.espClear() end)

SL.VisualsTab:Section("Прицел")
SL.xh = {frame = nil, size = 10, gap = 4}
function SL.buildCrosshair()
    if SL.xh.frame then SL.xh.frame:Destroy() end
    local f = SL.New("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), ZIndex = 40,
    }, SL.ScreenGui)
    local sz, g, t = SL.xh.size, SL.xh.gap, 2
    local function line(x, y, w, h)
        local l = SL.New("Frame", {
            BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, y),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, h), ZIndex = 40,
        }, f)
        SL.BindAccent(l, "BackgroundColor3")
    end
    line(0, -(g + sz / 2), t, sz)
    line(0, g + sz / 2, t, sz)
    line(-(g + sz / 2), 0, sz, t)
    line(g + sz / 2, 0, sz, t)
    SL.xh.frame = f
end
SL.VisualsTab:Toggle("Кастомный прицел", false, function(on)
    if on then SL.buildCrosshair()
    elseif SL.xh.frame then SL.xh.frame:Destroy() SL.xh.frame = nil end
end)
SL.VisualsTab:Slider("Длина линий", 4, 30, 10, function(v) SL.xh.size = v if SL.xh.frame then SL.buildCrosshair() end end)
SL.VisualsTab:Slider("Зазор в центре", 0, 20, 4, function(v) SL.xh.gap = v if SL.xh.frame then SL.buildCrosshair() end end)


-- ==================== VISUALS: 30 НОВЫХ ФУНКЦИЙ ====================
-- Все эффекты ниже клиентские. Для Drawing-оверлеев нужен executor с Drawing API.
SL.VFX = {
    Tracers = false, Boxes = false, HeadDots = false, Names = false,
    HealthBars = false, Distances = false, TeamColors = false,
    Chams = false, LocalAura = false, OrbitRing = false,
    JumpCircle = false, LandingRipple = false, FootstepRings = false,
    SpeedTrail = false, JumpTrail = false, CharacterGlow = false,
    OutlineOnly = false, Vignette = false, ScreenTint = false,
    ExtraBloom = false, SoftBlur = false, AmbientParticles = false,
    StarParticles = false, Snow = false, ExtraFog = false,
    ExtraSunRays = false, NeonWorld = false, DotCrosshair = false,
    PulseCrosshair = false, CameraFocus = false,
}
SL.VFXDrawings = {}
SL.VFXHighlights = {}
SL.VFXParts = {}
SL.VFXConns = {}
SL.VFXGui = {}
SL.VFXEffects = {}
SL.VFXLastStep = 0
SL.VFXCharacterConn = nil

function SL.VFXDrawing(kind)
    if not Drawing then return nil end
    local ok, obj = pcall(function() return Drawing.new(kind) end)
    if ok then
        table.insert(SL.VFXDrawings, obj)
        return obj
    end
    return nil
end

function SL.VFXRemoveDrawings()
    for _, obj in ipairs(SL.VFXDrawings) do pcall(function() obj:Remove() end) end
    table.clear(SL.VFXDrawings)
end

function SL.VFXDestroyParts()
    for _, obj in ipairs(SL.VFXParts) do pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
    table.clear(SL.VFXParts)
end

function SL.VFXMakeRing(position, radius, color, duration, thickness)
    local pieces = 32
    local folder = SL.New("Folder", {Name = "ScriptLoader_Ring"}, workspace)
    table.insert(SL.VFXParts, folder)
    for i = 1, pieces do
        local a1 = (i - 1) / pieces * math.pi * 2
        local a2 = i / pieces * math.pi * 2
        local p1 = Vector3.new(math.cos(a1) * radius, 0.12, math.sin(a1) * radius)
        local p2 = Vector3.new(math.cos(a2) * radius, 0.12, math.sin(a2) * radius)
        local mid = (p1 + p2) / 2
        local seg = SL.New("Part", {
            Name = "RingSegment", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanTouch = false,
            CanQuery = false, CastShadow = false, Material = Enum.Material.Neon,
            Color = color, Size = Vector3.new(thickness or 0.12, 0.08, (p2 - p1).Magnitude + 0.08),
            CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(position + mid, position + p2),
        }, folder)
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        local start = tick()
        while folder.Parent and tick() - start < duration do
            local alpha = math.clamp((tick() - start) / duration, 0, 1)
            for _, p in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                    p.Transparency = alpha
                    p.Size = Vector3.new(thickness or 0.12, 0.08, p.Size.Z)
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.04)
        end
        if folder.Parent then folder:Destroy() end
    end)
end

function SL.VFXSetEffect(key, className, props, enabled)
    local old = SL.VFXEffects[key]
    if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) SL.VFXEffects[key] = nil end
    if enabled then
        local effect = Instance.new(className)
        effect.Name = "ScriptLoader_" .. key
        for k, v in pairs(props) do pcall(function() effect[k] = v end) end
        effect.Parent = SL.Lighting
        SL.VFXEffects[key] = effect
    end
end

function SL.VFXSetScreenLayer(key, color, transparency)
    if SL.VFXGui[key] then SL.VFXGui[key]:Destroy() SL.VFXGui[key] = nil end
    if not color then return end
    local frame = SL.New("Frame", {
        Name = "ScriptLoader_" .. key, BackgroundColor3 = color,
        BackgroundTransparency = transparency, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), ZIndex = 35,
    }, SL.ScreenGui)
    SL.VFXGui[key] = frame
end

function SL.VFXGetPlayerColor(player)
    if player.Team and player.TeamColor then return player.TeamColor.Color end
    return Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 110)
end

-- Drawing overlay objects are allocated per player and reused.
function SL.VFXCreatePlayerDrawings()
    local entry = {
        tracer = SL.VFXDrawing("Line"),
        box1 = SL.VFXDrawing("Line"), box2 = SL.VFXDrawing("Line"),
        box3 = SL.VFXDrawing("Line"), box4 = SL.VFXDrawing("Line"),
        head = SL.VFXDrawing("Circle"), name = SL.VFXDrawing("Text"),
        healthBack = SL.VFXDrawing("Line"), health = SL.VFXDrawing("Line"),
        distance = SL.VFXDrawing("Text"),
    }
    return entry
end

function SL.VFXHideEntry(e)
    if not e then return end
    for _, obj in pairs(e) do
        if obj then pcall(function() obj.Visible = false end) end
    end
end

function SL.VFXUpdatePlayerOverlay()
    if not (SL.VFX.Tracers or SL.VFX.Boxes or SL.VFX.HeadDots or SL.VFX.Names or SL.VFX.HealthBars or SL.VFX.Distances) then
        for _, e in pairs(SL.VFXDrawings) do pcall(function() e.Visible = false end) end
        return
    end
    if not Drawing then return end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    local center = Vector2.new(cam.ViewportSize.X / 2, cam.ViewportSize.Y)
    local active = {}
    for _, player in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= SL.LocalPlayer then
            local char = player.Character
            local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if root and head and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                active[player] = true
                local e = SL.VFXHighlights[player]
                if not e or not e.tracer then
                    e = SL.VFXCreatePlayerDrawings()
                    SL.VFXHighlights[player] = e
                end
                local top, topOn = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.55, 0))
                local bottom, bottomOn = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position - Vector3.new(0, 2.8, 0))
                local headPos, headOn = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position)
                local visible = topOn and bottomOn and headOn
                if visible then
                    local col = SL.VFX.TeamColors and SL.VFXGetPlayerColor(player) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 110)
                    local h = math.max(8, math.abs(bottom.Y - top.Y))
                    local w = h * 0.48
                    local x, y = top.X - w / 2, top.Y
                    if e.tracer then
                        e.tracer.From = center
                        e.tracer.To = Vector2.new(root.Position.X, root.Position.Y)
                        local rp = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)
                        e.tracer.To = Vector2.new(rp.X, rp.Y)
                        e.tracer.Color, e.tracer.Thickness = col, 1.5
                        e.tracer.Visible = SL.VFX.Tracers
                    end
                    local corners = {
                        {Vector2.new(x, y), Vector2.new(x + w, y)},
                        {Vector2.new(x + w, y), Vector2.new(x + w, y + h)},
                        {Vector2.new(x + w, y + h), Vector2.new(x, y + h)},
                        {Vector2.new(x, y + h), Vector2.new(x, y)},
                    }
                    for i = 1, 4 do
                        local line = e["box" .. i]
                        if line then
                            line.From, line.To, line.Color = corners[i][1], corners[i][2], col
                            line.Thickness, line.Visible = 1.5, SL.VFX.Boxes
                        end
                    end
                    if e.head then
                        e.head.Position = Vector2.new(headPos.X, headPos.Y)
                        e.head.Radius, e.head.Color = 4, col
                        e.head.Filled, e.head.Visible = true, SL.VFX.HeadDots
                    end
                    if e.name then
                        e.name.Position = Vector2.new(top.X, y - 15)
                        e.name.Text, e.name.Center = player.DisplayName, true
                        e.name.Size, e.name.Color = 13, col
                        e.name.Outline, e.name.Visible = true, SL.VFX.Names
                    end
                    local ratio = math.clamp(hum.Health / math.max(hum.MaxHealth, 1), 0, 1)
                    if e.healthBack then
                        e.healthBack.From, e.healthBack.To = Vector2.new(x - 5, y), Vector2.new(x - 5, y + h)
                        e.healthBack.Color, e.healthBack.Thickness = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35), 3
                        e.healthBack.Visible = SL.VFX.HealthBars
                    end
                    if e.health then
                        e.health.From, e.health.To = Vector2.new(x - 5, y + h), Vector2.new(x - 5, y + h * (1 - ratio))
                        e.health.Color, e.health.Thickness = Color3.fromRGB(80, 230, 120), 2
                        e.health.Visible = SL.VFX.HealthBars
                    end
                    local myRoot = SL.GetRoot()
                    local dist = myRoot and math.floor((myRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude) or 0
                    if e.distance then
                        e.distance.Position = Vector2.new(top.X, bottom.Y + 2)
                        e.distance.Text, e.distance.Center = tostring(dist) .. "m", true
                        e.distance.Size, e.distance.Color = 12, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                        e.distance.Outline, e.distance.Visible = true, SL.VFX.Distances
                    end
                else
                    SL.VFXHideEntry(e)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for player, e in pairs(SL.VFXHighlights) do
        if typeof(player) == "Instance" and player:IsA("Player") and not active[player] then
            SL.VFXHideEntry(e)
            for _, obj in pairs(e) do pcall(function() obj:Remove() end) end
            SL.VFXHighlights[player] = nil
        end
    end
end

table.insert(SL.VFXConns, SL.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if tick() - SL.VFXLastStep < 0.025 then return end
    SL.VFXLastStep = tick()
    SL.VFXUpdatePlayerOverlay()
    local root = SL.GetRoot()
    if root then
        local t = tick()
        local aura = SL.VFXEffects.LocalAuraPart
        if SL.VFX.LocalAura then
            if not aura or not aura.Parent then
                aura = SL.New("Part", {
                    Name = "ScriptLoader_LocalAura", Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball,
                    Size = Vector3.new(7, 7, 7), Material = Enum.Material.ForceField,
                    Color = SL.Theme.Accent, Transparency = 0.72, Anchored = true,
                    CanCollide = false, CanTouch = false, CanQuery = false,
                }, workspace)
                SL.VFXEffects.LocalAuraPart = aura
            end
            aura.CFrame = root.CFrame
        elseif aura then aura:Destroy() SL.VFXEffects.LocalAuraPart = nil end

        local SL.orbit = SL.VFXEffects.OrbitParts
        if SL.VFX.OrbitRing then
            if not SL.orbit then
                SL.orbit = {}
                for i = 1, 24 do
                    SL.orbit[i] = SL.New("Part", {
                        Name = "ScriptLoader_Orbit", Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball,
                        Size = Vector3.new(0.22, 0.22, 0.22), Material = Enum.Material.Neon,
                        Color = SL.Theme.Accent, Anchored = true, CanCollide = false,
                        CanTouch = false, CanQuery = false,
                    }, workspace)
                end
                SL.VFXEffects.OrbitParts = SL.orbit
            end
            for i, p in ipairs(SL.orbit) do
                local a = t * 1.6 + i / #SL.orbit * math.pi * 2
                p.Position = root.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * 3.5, 1.2 + math.sin(a * 2) * 0.35, math.sin(a) * 3.5)
            end
        elseif SL.orbit then
            for _, p in ipairs(SL.orbit) do p:Destroy() end
            SL.VFXEffects.OrbitParts = nil
        end

        if SL.VFX.FootstepRings and root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 2 and math.random() < 0.08 then
            SL.VFXMakeRing(root.Position - Vector3.new(0, 2.8, 0), 1.1, SL.Theme.Accent, 0.45, 0.08)
        end
    end
end))

function SL.VFXBindCharacter(char)
    if SL.VFXCharacterConn then SL.VFXCharacterConn:Disconnect() SL.VFXCharacterConn = nil end
    local hum = char and char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    local root = char and char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
    if not hum or not root then return end
    local lastState = hum:GetState()
    SL.VFXCharacterConn = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, state)
        if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then
            if SL.VFX.JumpCircle then SL.VFXMakeRing(root.Position - Vector3.new(0, 2.7, 0), 2.4, SL.Theme.Accent, 0.65, 0.14) end
            if SL.VFX.JumpTrail then
                local a = SL.New("Attachment", {}, root)
                local b = SL.New("Attachment", {Position = Vector3.new(0, -1.5, 0)}, root)
                local trail = SL.New("Trail", {
                    Attachment0 = a, Attachment1 = b, Lifetime = 0.35,
                    Color = ColorSequence.new(SL.Theme.Accent), LightEmission = 0.8,
                    Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.15, 1), WidthScale = NumberSequence.new(0.7, 0),
                }, root)
                task.delay(0.5, function() trail:Destroy() a:Destroy() b:Destroy() end)
            end
        elseif state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed then
            if SL.VFX.LandingRipple then SL.VFXMakeRing(root.Position - Vector3.new(0, 2.7, 0), 3, Color3.fromRGB(130, 200, 255), 0.8, 0.16) end
        end
        lastState = state
    end)
end
if SL.LocalPlayer.Character then task.spawn(SL.VFXBindCharacter, SL.LocalPlayer.Character) end
table.insert(SL.VFXConns, SL.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(SL.VFXBindCharacter))

    SL.VFXSetEffect("ExtraSunRays", "SunRaysEffect", {Intensity = 0.12, Spread = 0.8}, v)
end)

    SL.VFX.NeonWorld = v
    SL.VFXSetEffect("NeonWorld", "ColorCorrectionEffect", {
        Saturation = 0.35, Contrast = 0.12, TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(220, 235, 255),
    }, v)
end)
SL.VisualsTab:Toggle("29 · Center Dot", false, function(v)
    SL.VFX.DotCrosshair = v
    if SL.VFXGui.CenterDot then SL.VFXGui.CenterDot:Destroy() SL.VFXGui.CenterDot = nil end
    if v then
        local dot = SL.New("Frame", {
            Name = "ScriptLoader_CenterDot", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4),
            BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 55,
        }, SL.ScreenGui)
        SL.Round(dot, 4)
        SL.VFXGui.CenterDot = dot
    end
end)
SL.VisualsTab:Toggle("30 · Pulse Crosshair", false, function(v)
    SL.VFX.PulseCrosshair = v
    if SL.VFXGui.PulseDot then SL.VFXGui.PulseDot:Destroy() SL.VFXGui.PulseDot = nil end
    if v then
        local dot = SL.New("Frame", {
            Name = "ScriptLoader_PulseDot", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8),
            BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.15,
            BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 56,
        }, SL.ScreenGui)
        SL.Round(dot, 8)
        SL.VFXGui.PulseDot = dot
        task.spawn(function()
            while SL.VFX.PulseCrosshair and dot.Parent do
                SL.Tween(dot, 0.45, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 13, 0, 13), BackgroundTransparency = 0.55})
                task.wait(0.45)
                if not (SL.VFX.PulseCrosshair and dot.Parent) then break end
                SL.Tween(dot, 0.45, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6), BackgroundTransparency = 0.1})
                task.wait(0.45)
            end
        end)
    end
end)

SL.OnUnload(function()
    if SL.VFXCharacterConn then SL.VFXCharacterConn:Disconnect() SL.VFXCharacterConn = nil end
    for _, c in ipairs(SL.VFXConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    table.clear(SL.VFXConns)
    SL.VFXRemoveDrawings()
    SL.VFXDestroyParts()
    for _, obj in pairs(SL.VFXHighlights) do pcall(function() if typeof(obj) == "Instance" then obj:Destroy() end end) end
    table.clear(SL.VFXHighlights)
    for _, obj in pairs(SL.VFXGui) do pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
    table.clear(SL.VFXGui)
    for _, obj in pairs(SL.VFXEffects) do
        pcall(function()
            if typeof(obj) == "Instance" then
                if obj:IsA("Folder") then
                    for _, child in ipairs(obj:GetChildren()) do child:Destroy() end
                end
                obj:Destroy()
            elseif type(obj) == "table" then
                for _, child in ipairs(obj) do child:Destroy() end
            end
        end)
    end
    table.clear(SL.VFXEffects)
    local c = SL.LocalPlayer.Character
    if c then
        for _, d in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
            if d.Name:find("^ScriptLoader_") then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
        end
    end
end)



-- ==================== VISUALS: 15 ДОПОЛНИТЕЛЬНЫХ ФУНКЦИЙ ====================
SL.VisualsTab:Section("Дополнительная камера и цвет")
SL.extraVisualEffects = {}
function SL.ExtraVisualEffect(key, className, props, enabled)
    local effect = SL.extraVisualEffects[key]
    if enabled then
        if not effect or not effect.Parent then
            effect = Instance.new(className)
            effect.Name = "ScriptLoader_Extra_" .. key
            for prop, value in pairs(props) do effect[prop] = value end
            effect.Parent = SL.Lighting
            SL.extraVisualEffects[key] = effect
        end
    elseif effect then
        effect:Destroy()
        SL.extraVisualEffects[key] = nil
    end
end
SL.VisualsTab:Toggle("Цветовой тон: розовый", false, function(v)
    SL.ExtraVisualEffect("PinkTone", "ColorCorrectionEffect", {TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 225), Saturation = 0.18}, v)

    if not ok then
        warn("[ScriptHub] C00lGUI: " .. tostring(err))
        SL.Notify("C00lGUI недоступен", "Внешний скрипт не загрузился. Открыл встроенные функции (Fly, гравитация, TP).", 5, true)
        SL.SelectTab("Movement")
    end
end)


-- Локальные развлекательные эффекты (не влияют на других игроков)
SL.FunTab:Section("Локальные эффекты")
SL.FunLighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
SL.FunRunService = game:GetService("RunService")
SL.FunActive = {}
SL.FunOriginalFov = nil

function SL.FunCleanup(key)
    local item = SL.FunActive[key]
    if not item then return end
    if key == "FovBreathing" or key == "ZoomWave" then
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam and SL.FunOriginalFov then cam.FieldOfView = SL.FunOriginalFov end
        SL.FunOriginalFov = nil
    end
    if item.conn then pcall(function() item.conn:Disconnect() end) end
    if item.object then pcall(function() item.object:Destroy() end) end
    if item.objects then
        for _, obj in ipairs(item.objects) do pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
    end
    SL.FunActive[key] = nil
end

function SL.FunEffectToggle(label, key, create)
    SL.FunTab:Toggle(label, false, function(on)
        SL.FunCleanup(key)
        if on then
            local ok, result = pcall(create)
            if ok and result then
                SL.FunActive[key] = result
            else
                SL.Notify(label, "Не удалось включить эффект: " .. tostring(result), 4, true)
            end
        end
    end)
end

function SL.FunLightingToggle(label, key, className, properties)
    SL.FunEffectToggle(label, key, function()
        local effect = Instance.new(className)
        effect.Name = "ScriptLoader_Fun_" .. key
        for property, value in pairs(properties) do
            effect[property] = value
        end
        effect.Parent = SL.FunLighting
        return {object = effect}
    end)
end

SL.FunEffectToggle("Режим дозы (переливы + покачивание)", "DoseMode", function()
    local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
    cc.Name = "ScriptLoader_Fun_DoseColor"
    cc.Saturation, cc.Contrast, cc.Brightness = 0.35, 0.08, 0.02
    cc.Parent = SL.FunLighting

    local bloom = Instance.new("BloomEffect")
    bloom.Name = "ScriptLoader_Fun_DoseBloom"
    bloom.Intensity, bloom.Size, bloom.Threshold = 0.7, 28, 1.1
    bloom.Parent = SL.FunLighting

    local blur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
    blur.Name = "ScriptLoader_Fun_DoseBlur"
    blur.Size = 2
    blur.Parent = SL.FunLighting

    local started = tick()
    local conn = SL.FunRunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        local t = tick() - started
        cc.TintColor = Color3.fromHSV((t * 0.055) % 1, 0.48, 1)
        cc.Saturation = 0.25 + math.sin(t * 0.7) * 0.12
        bloom.Intensity = 0.55 + (math.sin(t * 1.1) + 1) * 0.12
        blur.Size = 1.5 + (math.sin(t * 0.8) + 1) * 0.8
        cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(
            math.rad(math.sin(t * 0.65) * 0.12),
            math.rad(math.sin(t * 0.42) * 0.16),
            math.rad(math.sin(t * 0.55) * 0.28)
        )
    end)
    return {conn = conn, objects = {cc, bloom, blur}}
end)

    local started = tick()

        local current = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if current then current.FieldOfView = (SL.FunOriginalFov or 70) + math.sin((tick() - started) * 1.1) * 3 end
    end)
    return {conn = conn}
end)

SL.FunEffectToggle("🌊 Волна экрана (качает камеру)", "ScreenWave", function()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local t = 0
    local origCf = cam.CFrame
    local conn = SL.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        t += dt
        if cam.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
            cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame
                * CFrame.Angles(math.sin(t * 2.1) * 0.018, math.sin(t * 1.7) * 0.012, math.sin(t * 3) * 0.008)
        end
    end)
    return {conn = conn}
end)

end)

SL.FunEffectToggle("🌙 Сон (плавное угасание-восстановление)", "DreamFade", function()
    local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
    cc.Name = "SL_Dream"
    cc.Parent = SL.FunLighting
    local t = 0
    local conn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        t += dt
        local wave = (math.sin(t * 0.5) + 1) / 2
        cc.Brightness = -0.35 * wave
        cc.Saturation = 0.3 - 0.6 * wave
        cc.TintColor = Color3.fromHSV(0.6 + wave * 0.05, 0.2 * wave, 1)
    end)
    return {object = cc, conn = conn}
end)

SL.FunEffectToggle("🔴 Красная тревога (мигание)", "RedAlert", function()
    local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
    cc.Name = "SL_Alert"
    cc.Parent = SL.FunLighting
    local snd = SL.New("Sound", {SoundId="rbxassetid://131961136", Volume=0.3, Looped=true, RollOffMaxDistance=9999}, workspace.CurrentCamera)
    snd:Play()
    local t = 0
    local conn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        t += dt
        local pulse = math.max(0, math.sin(t * 3.14))
        cc.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, math.floor(200*(1-pulse*0.7)), math.floor(200*(1-pulse*0.7)))
        cc.Brightness = -0.1 * pulse
        cc.Contrast = 0.2 * pulse
    end)
    return {objects = {cc, snd}, conn = conn}
end)

SL.FunEffectToggle("⚡ Электро (неоновый шторм)", "Electro", function()
    local folder = SL.New("Folder", {Name="SL_Electro"}, workspace)
    local t = 0
    local conn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        t += dt
        local r = SL.GetRoot()
        if not r or math.random() > 0.4 then return end
        local part = SL.New("Part", {
            Size=Vector3.new(0.08, math.random(3,8), 0.08), Anchored=true,
            CanCollide=false, CanTouch=false, CanQuery=false, CastShadow=false,
            Material=Enum.Material.Neon, Color=Color3.fromRGB(100+math.random(0,155), 160+math.random(0,95), 255),
            Transparency=0.1,
        }, folder)
        local base = r.Position + Vector3.new(math.random(-6,6), math.random(0,8), math.random(-6,6))
        part.CFrame = CFrame.new(base) * CFrame.Angles(math.random()*math.pi, math.random()*math.pi, 0)
        task.spawn(function()
            for i=1,6 do
                if not part.Parent then break end
                part.Transparency = i/6
                task.wait(0.025)
            end
            part:Destroy()
        end)
    end)
    return {object = folder, conn = conn}
end)

SL.FunEffectToggle("🌸 Лепестки сакуры", "Sakura", function()
    local folder = SL.New("Folder", {Name="SL_Sakura"}, workspace)
    local conn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local r = SL.GetRoot()
        if not r or math.random() > 0.25 then return end
        local colors = {
            Color3.fromRGB(255,182,193), Color3.fromRGB(255,160,180),
            Color3.fromRGB(255,200,210), Color3.fromRGB(255,140,170),
        }
        local part = SL.New("Part", {
            Size=Vector3.new(0.3,0.05,0.3), Anchored=false,
            CanCollide=false, CanTouch=false, CanQuery=false, CastShadow=false,
            Material=Enum.Material.Neon, Color=colors[math.random(#colors)], Transparency=0.1,
        }, folder)
        part.CFrame = CFrame.new(r.Position + Vector3.new(math.random(-8,8), 12, math.random(-8,8)))
        part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-2,2)*0.5, -3, math.random(-2,2)*0.5)
        part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(math.random(), math.random(), math.random())
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(3)
            for i=1,8 do
                if not part.Parent then break end
                part.Transparency = i/8
                task.wait(0.08)
            end
            part:Destroy()
        end)
    end)
    return {object = folder, conn = conn}
end)

SL.FunEffectToggle("💀 Кровь (красные брызги)", "Blood", function()
    local folder = SL.New("Folder", {Name="SL_Blood"}, workspace)
    local hum = SL.GetHum()
    local lastHP = hum and hum.Health or 100
    local conn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local h, r = SL.GetHum(), SL.GetRoot()
        if not (h and r) then return end
        local dmg = lastHP - h.Health
        lastHP = h.Health
        local n = dmg > 0 and math.ceil(dmg / 5) or (math.random() > 0.96 and 1 or 0)
        for _ = 1, n do
            local part = SL.New("Part", {
                Size=Vector3.new(0.15,0.15,0.15), Anchored=false,
                CanCollide=false, CanTouch=false, CanQuery=false, CastShadow=false,
                Material=Enum.Material.Neon, Color=Color3.fromRGB(180+math.random(0,75), 0, 0),
                Transparency=0.0,
            }, folder)
            part.CFrame = CFrame.new(r.Position + Vector3.new(math.random(-1,1), math.random(0,2), math.random(-1,1)))
            part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-8,8), math.random(4,12), math.random(-8,8))
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(0.6)
                for i=1,8 do if not part.Parent then break end part.Transparency=i/8 task.wait(0.05) end
                part:Destroy()
            end)
        end
    end)
    return {object=folder, conn=conn}
end)

SL.FunEffectToggle("🎆 Конфетти при прыжке", "Confetti", function()
    local folder = SL.New("Folder", {Name="SL_Confetti"}, workspace)
    local wasAir = false
    local conn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local h, r = SL.GetHum(), SL.GetRoot()
        if not (h and r) then return end
        local inAir = h.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
        if inAir and not wasAir then
            for _ = 1, 20 do
                local part = SL.New("Part", {
                    Size=Vector3.new(0.2,0.2,0.2), Anchored=false,
                    CanCollide=false, CanTouch=false, CanQuery=false, CastShadow=false,
                    Material=Enum.Material.Neon, Color=Color3.fromHSV(math.random(), 1, 1),
                    Transparency=0,
                }, folder)
                part.CFrame = CFrame.new(r.Position + Vector3.new(math.random(-2,2), 0, math.random(-2,2)))
                part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-12,12), math.random(8,20), math.random(-12,12))
                part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-5,5), math.random(-5,5), math.random(-5,5))
                task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(1.2)
                    for i=1,8 do if not part.Parent then break end part.Transparency=i/8 task.wait(0.05) end
                    part:Destroy()
                end)
            end
        end
        wasAir = inAir
    end)
    return {object=folder, conn=conn}
end)

SL.FunTab:Button("Сбросить эффекты Fun", function()
    for key in pairs(SL.FunActive) do SL.FunCleanup(key) end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam and SL.FunOriginalFov then cam.FieldOfView = SL.FunOriginalFov end
    SL.FunOriginalFov = nil
    SL.Notify("Fun", "Локальные эффекты отключены", 3)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function()
    for key in pairs(SL.FunActive) do SL.FunCleanup(key) end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam and SL.FunOriginalFov then cam.FieldOfView = SL.FunOriginalFov end
    SL.FunOriginalFov = nil
end)

        Name = "ScriptLoaderClock", BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Top, BackgroundTransparency = 0.15,
        TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 14,

        Size = UDim2.new(0, 180, 0, 32), ZIndex = 60,

    SL.Round(SL.miscClockGui, 8)
    SL.miscClockConn = SL.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if SL.miscClockGui then SL.miscClockGui.Text = (function() local t=os.time() return string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d",math.floor(t/3600)%24,math.floor(t/60)%60,t%60) end)() end
    end)
end)

SL.playerNameplateSaved = nil
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Скрыть свою табличку имени", false, function(on)
    local h = SL.GetHum()
    if not h then return end
    if on then
        SL.playerNameplateSaved = h.DisplayDistanceType
        h.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
    elseif SL.playerNameplateSaved then
        h.DisplayDistanceType = SL.playerNameplateSaved
        SL.playerNameplateSaved = nil
    end
end)
SL.OnUnload(function()
    local h = SL.GetHum()
    if h and SL.playerNameplateSaved then h.DisplayDistanceType = SL.playerNameplateSaved end
    SL.playerNameplateSaved = nil
end)

SL.playerAccessorySaved = {}
function SL.SetOwnAccessoriesHidden(hidden)
    local char = SL.GetChar()
    if not char then return end
    for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Parent and obj.Parent:IsA("Accessory") then
            if hidden then
                if SL.playerAccessorySaved[obj] == nil then SL.playerAccessorySaved[obj] = obj.LocalTransparencyModifier end
                obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
            elseif SL.playerAccessorySaved[obj] ~= nil then
                obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = SL.playerAccessorySaved[obj]
                SL.playerAccessorySaved[obj] = nil
            end
        end
    end
end
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Скрыть свои аксессуары (локально)", false, function(on)
    if on then
        SL.SetOwnAccessoriesHidden(true)
    else
        SL.SetOwnAccessoriesHidden(false)
    end
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() SL.SetOwnAccessoriesHidden(false) table.clear(SL.playerAccessorySaved) end)

SL.playerFovSaved = nil
SL.PlayerTab:Slider("Поле зрения камеры", 50, 100, 70, function(value)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then
        if not SL.playerFovSaved then SL.playerFovSaved = cam.FieldOfView end
        cam.FieldOfView = value
    end
end)
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Вернуть стандартный FOV", function()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then cam.FieldOfView = 70 end
    SL.playerFovSaved = nil
end)
SL.OnUnload(function()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam and SL.playerFovSaved then cam.FieldOfView = SL.playerFovSaved end
    SL.playerFovSaved = nil
end)
SL.PlayerTab:Label("Изменения внешнего вида в этой вкладке видны только на твоём клиенте.")

SL.miscStatsGui, SL.miscStatsConn = nil, nil
SL.MiscTab:Toggle("FPS на экране", false, function(on)
    if SL.miscStatsConn then SL.miscStatsConn:Disconnect() SL.miscStatsConn = nil end
    if SL.miscStatsGui then SL.miscStatsGui:Destroy() SL.miscStatsGui = nil end
    if not on then return end
    SL.miscStatsGui = SL.New("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Top, BackgroundTransparency = 0.12, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 13, Text = "FPS: --",
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12), Size = UDim2.new(0, 110, 0, 28), ZIndex = 60,
    }, SL.ScreenGui)
    SL.Round(SL.miscStatsGui, 8)
    local frames, elapsed = 0, 0
    SL.miscStatsConn = SL.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        frames, elapsed = frames + 1, elapsed + dt
        if elapsed >= 0.5 then
            SL.miscStatsGui.Text = "FPS: " .. math.floor(frames / elapsed + 0.5)
            frames, elapsed = 0, 0
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.miscStatsConn then SL.miscStatsConn:Disconnect() end if SL.miscStatsGui then SL.miscStatsGui:Destroy() end end)

SL.miscPingGui, SL.miscPingConn = nil, nil
SL.MiscTab:Toggle("Ping на экране", false, function(on)
    if SL.miscPingConn then SL.miscPingConn:Disconnect() SL.miscPingConn = nil end
    if SL.miscPingGui then SL.miscPingGui:Destroy() SL.miscPingGui = nil end
    if not on then return end
    SL.miscPingGui = SL.New("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Top, BackgroundTransparency = 0.12, TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 13, Text = "Ping: --",
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 44), Size = UDim2.new(0, 125, 0, 28), ZIndex = 60,
    }, SL.ScreenGui)
    SL.Round(SL.miscPingGui, 8)
    local elapsed = 0
    SL.miscPingConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        elapsed = elapsed + dt
        if elapsed >= 1 then
            local ok, value = pcall(function() return SL.Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValueString() end)
            if SL.miscPingGui then SL.miscPingGui.Text = "Ping: " .. (ok and tostring(value) or "N/A") end
            elapsed = 0
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.miscPingConn then SL.miscPingConn:Disconnect() end if SL.miscPingGui then SL.miscPingGui:Destroy() end end)

    end
    local a = SL.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(updateCount)

    local c = SL.GetChar()

            if obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("Beam") then obj.Enabled = not on end
        end
    end
end)

    local c = SL.GetChar()
    local existing = c and c:FindFirstChild("ScriptLoaderOwnHighlight")
    if on and c and not existing then
        SL.New("Highlight", {Name = "ScriptLoaderOwnHighlight", FillColor = SL.Theme.Accent,
            OutlineColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), FillTransparency = 0.75}, c)
    elseif not on and existing then
        existing:Destroy()

    local c = SL.GetChar()

    if h then h:Destroy() end

    local c = SL.GetChar()
    if c then
        for _, obj in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                obj.CastShadow = true
            elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("Beam") then
                obj.Enabled = true
            end
        end
        local h = c:FindFirstChild("ScriptLoaderOwnHighlight")
        if h then h:Destroy() end
    end
    SL.Notify("Персонаж", "Локальные визуальные настройки сброшены", 3)
end)


-- ==================== PLAYER: ДОПОЛНИТЕЛЬНЫЕ ФУНКЦИИ ====================

SL.PlayerTab:Section("Внешний вид (локально)")

-- Цвет всех частей персонажа
SL.bodyColorConn = nil
SL.savedBodyColors = {}
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Кастомный цвет тела", false, function(on)
    for part, col in pairs(SL.savedBodyColors) do
        if part and part.Parent then part.Color = col end
    end
    SL.savedBodyColors = {}
    if not on then return end
    local c = SL.GetChar()
    if not c then return end
    local col = Color3.fromHSV(math.random(), 0.8, 0.9)
    for _, p in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
        if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
            SL.savedBodyColors[p] = p.Color
            p.Color = col
        end
    end
end)
SL.OnUnload(function()
    for part, col in pairs(SL.savedBodyColors) do
        if part and part.Parent then part.Color = col end
    end
end)
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Случайный цвет тела", function()
    local c = SL.GetChar()
    if not c then return end
    local col = Color3.fromHSV(math.random(), 0.85, 0.9)
    for _, p in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
        if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
            if not SL.savedBodyColors[p] then SL.savedBodyColors[p] = p.Color end
            p.Color = col
        end
    end
    SL.Notify("Персонаж", "Цвет тела изменён", 2)
end)

-- Rainbow тело
SL.rainbowBodyConn = nil
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Радужное тело (анимация)", false, function(on)
    if SL.rainbowBodyConn then SL.rainbowBodyConn:Disconnect() SL.rainbowBodyConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    local hue = 0
    SL.rainbowBodyConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        hue = (hue + dt * 0.4) % 1
        local c = SL.GetChar()
        if not c then return end
        for _, p in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
            if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                p.Color = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.9, 0.95)
            end
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.rainbowBodyConn then SL.rainbowBodyConn:Disconnect() end end)

-- Невидимое тело (только свой персонаж)
SL.invisConn = nil
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Невидимый (тело, локально)", false, function(on)
    if SL.invisConn then SL.invisConn:Disconnect() SL.invisConn = nil end
    if not on then
        local c = SL.GetChar()
        if c then
            for _, p in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end
            end
        end
        return
    end
    SL.invisConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local c = SL.GetChar()
        if c then
            for _, p in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                    p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.invisConn then SL.invisConn:Disconnect() end end)

-- Размер персонажа
SL.sizeConn, SL.charScale = nil, 1
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Кастомный размер персонажа", false, function(on)
    if SL.sizeConn then SL.sizeConn:Disconnect() SL.sizeConn = nil end
    if not on then
        local c = SL.GetChar()
        if c then
            local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h then
                h.BodyDepthScale.Value  = 1
                h.BodyHeightScale.Value = 1
                h.BodyWidthScale.Value  = 1
                h.HeadScale.Value       = 1
            end
        end
        return
    end
    SL.sizeConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local c = SL.GetChar()
        if not c then return end
        local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not h then return end
        h.BodyDepthScale.Value  = SL.charScale
        h.BodyHeightScale.Value = SL.charScale
        h.BodyWidthScale.Value  = SL.charScale
        h.HeadScale.Value       = SL.charScale
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function()
    if SL.sizeConn then SL.sizeConn:Disconnect() end
    local c = SL.GetChar()
    if c then
        local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if h then
            h.BodyDepthScale.Value, h.BodyHeightScale.Value = 1, 1
            h.BodyWidthScale.Value, h.HeadScale.Value       = 1, 1
        end
    end
end)
SL.PlayerTab:Slider("Размер персонажа", 0.1, 5, 1, function(v) SL.charScale = v end, 1)

-- Только большая голова
SL.bigHeadConn, SL.headScale2 = nil, 2
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Большая голова", false, function(on)
    if SL.bigHeadConn then SL.bigHeadConn:Disconnect() SL.bigHeadConn = nil end
    if not on then
        local c = SL.GetChar()
        if c then
            local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h then h.HeadScale.Value = 1 end
        end
        return
    end
    SL.bigHeadConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local c = SL.GetChar()
        local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if h then h.HeadScale.Value = SL.headScale2 end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function()
    if SL.bigHeadConn then SL.bigHeadConn:Disconnect() end
    local c = SL.GetChar()
    local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if h then h.HeadScale.Value = 1 end
end)
SL.PlayerTab:Slider("Размер головы", 0.5, 8, 2, function(v) SL.headScale2 = v end, 1)

-- Хитбокс персонажа (HumanoidRootPart)
SL.hbSaved = nil
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Уменьшить свой хитбокс (клиент)", false, function(on)
    local c = SL.GetChar()
    local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return end
    if on then
        SL.hbSaved = root.Size
        root.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1)
    elseif SL.hbSaved then
        root.Size = SL.hbSaved
        SL.hbSaved = nil
    end
end)
SL.OnUnload(function()
    if SL.hbSaved then
        local c = SL.GetChar()
        local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then root.Size = SL.hbSaved end
    end
end)

SL.PlayerTab:Section("Анимации")

-- Скорость анимаций
SL.animSpeedConn, SL.animSpeed = nil, 1
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Кастомная скорость анимаций", false, function(on)
    if SL.animSpeedConn then SL.animSpeedConn:Disconnect() SL.animSpeedConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    SL.animSpeedConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local c = SL.GetChar()
        if not c then return end
        local animate = c:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if animate then
            for _, v in ipairs(animate:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("Animation") then
                    for _, track in ipairs(c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"):GetPlayingAnimationTracks() or {}) do
                        track:AdjustSpeed(SL.animSpeed)
                    end
                    break
                end
            end
            return
        end
        local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if h then
            for _, track in ipairs(h:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                track:AdjustSpeed(SL.animSpeed)
            end
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.animSpeedConn then SL.animSpeedConn:Disconnect() end end)
SL.PlayerTab:Slider("Скорость анимаций", 0, 5, 1, function(v) SL.animSpeed = v end, 1)

-- Заморозить анимацию (speed = 0)
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Заморозить анимацию (стоп-кадр)", function()
    local c = SL.GetChar()
    local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not h then return end
    for _, track in ipairs(h:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
        track:AdjustSpeed(0)
    end
    SL.Notify("Анимация", "Заморожена", 2)
end)

SL.PlayerTab:Button("Возобновить анимации (x1)", function()
    local c = SL.GetChar()
    local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not h then return end
    for _, track in ipairs(h:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
        track:AdjustSpeed(1)
    end
    SL.Notify("Анимация", "Скорость x1", 2)
end)

-- Список играющих анимаций
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Показать играющие анимации", function()
    local c = SL.GetChar()
    local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not h then error("персонаж не найден", 0) end
    local tracks = h:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
    if #tracks == 0 then SL.Notify("Анимации", "Нет активных анимаций", 3) return end
    local lines = {}
    for _, t in ipairs(tracks) do
        table.insert(lines, t.Animation.AnimationId .. " (x" .. string.format("%.1f", t.Speed) .. ")")
    end
    local txt = table.concat(lines, "\n")
    if setclipboard then pcall(setclipboard, txt) end
    SL.Notify("Анимации", #tracks .. " активных (скопировано)", 4, true)
end)

    SL.LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0
    SL.Notify("Камера", "Дистанция сброшена", 2)
end)

-- Смещение камеры
SL.camOffConn, SL.camOffX, SL.camOffY = nil, 0, 0
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Смещение камеры (offset)", false, function(on)
    if SL.camOffConn then SL.camOffConn:Disconnect() SL.camOffConn = nil end
    if not on then
        workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame
        return
    end
    SL.camOffConn = SL.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame * CFrame.new(SL.camOffX, SL.camOffY, 0)
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.camOffConn then SL.camOffConn:Disconnect() end end)

SL.PlayerTab:Slider("Смещение Y", -5, 5, 0, function(v) SL.camOffY = v end, 1)

SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Камера через плечо (TPS)", false, function(on)
    if SL.shoulderConn then SL.shoulderConn:Disconnect() SL.shoulderConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    SL.shoulderConn = SL.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame * CFrame.new(SL.shoulderSide, 0, 0)
    end)
end)

SL.PlayerTab:Section("Статистика и инфо")

-- Общая дистанция пройденного пути
SL.distConn, SL.totalDist, SL.lastDistPos = nil, 0, nil
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Счётчик пройденного пути", false, function(on)
    if SL.distConn then SL.distConn:Disconnect() SL.distConn = nil end
    SL.totalDist, SL.lastDistPos = 0, nil
    if not on then return end
    SL.distConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local r = SL.GetRoot()
        if not r then return end
        if SL.lastDistPos then
            SL.totalDist += (r.Position - SL.lastDistPos).Magnitude
        end
        SL.lastDistPos = r.Position
    end)
end)
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Показать пройденный путь", function()
    SL.Notify("Путь", string.format("%.1f studs (≈ %.1f м)", SL.totalDist, SL.totalDist * 0.28), 4, true)
end)
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Сбросить счётчик пути", function()
    SL.totalDist, SL.lastDistPos = 0, nil
    SL.Notify("Путь", "Счётчик сброшен", 2)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.distConn then SL.distConn:Disconnect() end end)

-- Время в сессии
SL.sessionStart = tick()
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Время в игре (сессия)", function()
    local elapsed = tick() - SL.sessionStart
    local m = math.floor(elapsed / 60)
    local sc = math.floor(elapsed % 60)
    SL.Notify("Сессия", string.format("%d мин %d сек в игре", m, sc), 4, true)
end)

-- Счётчик смертей
SL.deathCount = 0
SL.deathCountConn = nil
SL.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    local h = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    if h then
        h.Died:Connect(function() SL.deathCount += 1 end)
    end
end)
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Показать счётчик смертей", function()
    SL.Notify("Смерти", "В этой сессии: " .. SL.deathCount, 3, true)
end)
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Сбросить счётчик смертей", function()
    SL.deathCount = 0
    SL.Notify("Смерти", "Сброшен", 2)
end)

-- Счётчик убийств (через Humanoid.Died других)
SL.killCount = 0
SL.killCountConns = {}
function SL.hookKillCount(p)
    if p == SL.LocalPlayer then return end
    local function hookChar(char)
        local h = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not h then return end
        local c = h.Died:Connect(function() SL.killCount += 1 end)
        table.insert(SL.killCountConns, c)
    end
    if p.Character then hookChar(p.Character) end
    local c = p.CharacterAdded:Connect(hookChar)
    table.insert(SL.killCountConns, c)
end
for _, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do SL.hookKillCount(p) end
SL.killPlayerConn = SL.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(SL.hookKillCount)
SL.OnUnload(function()
    for _, c in ipairs(SL.killCountConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    SL.killPlayerConn:Disconnect()
end)
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Показать счётчик убийств (клиент)", function()
    SL.Notify("Убийства", "Зафиксировано смертей других: " .. SL.killCount, 3, true)
end)
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Сбросить счётчик убийств", function()
    SL.killCount = 0
    SL.Notify("Убийства", "Сброшен", 2)
end)

SL.PlayerTab:Section("Быстрые действия")

-- Авто-ресет при застревании
SL.stuckConn, SL.stuckPos, SL.stuckTimer = nil, nil, 0
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Авто-ресет при застревании (5 сек)", false, function(on)
    if SL.stuckConn then SL.stuckConn:Disconnect() SL.stuckConn = nil end
    SL.stuckPos, SL.stuckTimer = nil, 0
    if not on then return end
    SL.stuckConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        local r, h = SL.GetRoot(), SL.GetHum()
        if not (r and h) or h.Health <= 0 then return end
        local pos = r.Position
        if SL.stuckPos and (pos - SL.stuckPos).Magnitude < 1 then
            SL.stuckTimer += dt
            if SL.stuckTimer >= 5 then
                SL.stuckTimer = 0
                h.Health = 0
                SL.Notify("Авто-ресет", "Застрял — перезапуск", 2)
            end
        else
            SL.stuckPos, SL.stuckTimer = pos, 0
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.stuckConn then SL.stuckConn:Disconnect() end end)

-- Авто-реконнект при падении HP ниже порога
SL.lowHPConn, SL.lowHPThresh = nil, 20
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Авто-ресет при низком HP", false, function(on)
    if SL.lowHPConn then SL.lowHPConn:Disconnect() SL.lowHPConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    SL.lowHPConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local h = SL.GetHum()
        if h and h.Health > 0 and h.Health < SL.lowHPThresh then
            h.Health = 0
            SL.Notify("Авто-ресет", "HP < " .. SL.lowHPThresh .. " — перезапуск", 2)
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.lowHPConn then SL.lowHPConn:Disconnect() end end)
SL.PlayerTab:Slider("Порог HP для авто-ресета", 1, 50, 20, function(v) SL.lowHPThresh = v end)

-- Авто-вставать (если лежит/сидит)
SL.autoStandConn = nil
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Авто-вставание (анти-сит)", false, function(on)
    if SL.autoStandConn then SL.autoStandConn:Disconnect() SL.autoStandConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    SL.autoStandConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local h = SL.GetHum()
        if h and h.Sit then h.Sit = false end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.autoStandConn then SL.autoStandConn:Disconnect() end end)

-- Выбросить в случайном направлении
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Выбросить себя в случайном направлении", function()
    local r = SL.GetRoot()
    if not r then return end
    local dir = Vector3.new(math.random()-0.5, math.random()*0.5+0.5, math.random()-0.5).Unit
    r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = dir * 120
    SL.Notify("Бросок", "Улетел!", 1)
end)

-- Телепорт на спавн
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Телепорт на точку спавна", function()
    local r = SL.GetRoot()
    if not r then return end
    local spawns = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpawnLocation")
    if spawns then
        r.CFrame = spawns.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
        SL.Notify("TP", "На спавн", 2)
    else
        r.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 10, 0)
        SL.Notify("TP", "Спавн не найден, TP на 0,0,0", 2)
    end
end)

        r.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 10, 0)
        SL.Notify("TP", "Центр карты (0, 10, 0)", 2)
    end
end)

SL.PlayerTab:Section("Другие игроки")

-- Показать инфо о всех
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Инфо о всех игроках на сервере", function()
    local lines = {}
    for i, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local me = p == SL.LocalPlayer and " (ты)" or ""
        local team = p.Team and " [" .. p.Team.Name .. "]" or ""
        table.insert(lines, i .. ". " .. p.Name .. me .. team)
    end
    local txt = table.concat(lines, "\n")
    if setclipboard then pcall(setclipboard, txt) end
    SL.Notify("Игроки", table.concat(lines, " | "):sub(1, 100), 5, true)
end)

-- Следить за HP всех (уведомление при чьей-то смерти)
SL.watchAllConn, SL.watchAllHooks = nil, {}
SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("Уведомлять о смерти любого игрока", false, function(on)
    for _, c in ipairs(SL.watchAllHooks) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    SL.watchAllHooks = {}
    SL.watchAllConn = nil
    if not on then return end
    local function hookP(p)
        if p == SL.LocalPlayer then return end
        local function hookChar(char)
            local h = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h then
                local c = h.Died:Connect(function()
                    SL.Notify("Смерть", p.Name .. " умер!", 3)
                end)
                table.insert(SL.watchAllHooks, c)
            end
        end
        if p.Character then hookChar(p.Character) end
        local c = p.CharacterAdded:Connect(hookChar)
        table.insert(SL.watchAllHooks, c)
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do hookP(p) end
    local c = SL.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hookP)
    table.insert(SL.watchAllHooks, c)
    SL.watchAllConn = c
end)
SL.OnUnload(function()
    for _, c in ipairs(SL.watchAllHooks) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
end)

-- Расстояния до всех игроков
SL.PlayerTab:Button("Показать дистанции до всех", function()
    local r = SL.GetRoot()
    if not r then error("нет персонажа", 0) end
    local lines = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= SL.LocalPlayer and p.Character then
            local tr = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if tr then
                local d = math.floor((r.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude)
                table.insert(lines, p.Name .. ": " .. d .. " studs")
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(lines, function(a, b)
        return tonumber(a:match("%d+")) < tonumber(b:match("%d+"))
    end)
    if #lines == 0 then SL.Notify("Дистанции", "Нет других игроков", 3) return end
    if setclipboard then pcall(setclipboard, table.concat(lines, "\n")) end
    SL.Notify("Ближайший", lines[1] .. " | всего " .. #lines .. " (скопировано)", 5, true)
end)

-- Текущий играющий трек и флаг зацикливания
SL.emotePlaying = nil
SL.emoteLoop = false
SL.emoteLoopConn = nil

function SL.stopCurrentEmote()
    if SL.emoteLoopConn then SL.emoteLoopConn:Disconnect() SL.emoteLoopConn = nil end
    if SL.emotePlaying then
        pcall(function() SL.emotePlaying:Stop(0.2) end)
        SL.emotePlaying = nil
    end
end
SL.OnUnload(SL.stopCurrentEmote)

function SL.playEmoteById(assetId)
    SL.stopCurrentEmote()
    local c = SL.GetChar()
    local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not h then error("персонаж не найден", 0) end
    local AnimCtrl = h.Animator or h
    local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
    anim.AnimationId = assetId
    local track = AnimCtrl:LoadAnimation(anim)
    track:Play()
    SL.emotePlaying = track
    if SL.emoteLoop then
        SL.emoteLoopConn = track.Stopped:Connect(function()
            if SL.emoteLoop then
                track:Play()
            end
        end)
    end
    return track
end

function SL.playEmoteByName(name)
    -- Пробуем через LocalScript Animate (FE-безопасный способ)
    local c = SL.GetChar()
    if not c then error("нет персонажа", 0) end
    local animate = c:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if animate then
        -- Метод 1: через скрипт Animate (работает в большинстве игр)
        local emoteEvent = animate:FindFirstChild("PlayEmote")
        if emoteEvent and emoteEvent:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            pcall(function() emoteEvent:FireServer(name) end)
            return
        end
        -- Метод 2: имитация /e команды через чат
        pcall(function()
            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
                :WaitForChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents", 2)
                :WaitForChild("SayMessageRequest", 2)
                :FireServer("/e " .. name, "All")
        end)
        return
    end
    error("Animate не найден, попробуй эмоцию по ID", 0)
end

-- Кнопка стоп
SL.PlayerTab:Button("⏹ Остановить эмоцию", function()
    SL.stopCurrentEmote()
    -- Восстановить обычные анимации
    local c = SL.GetChar()
    local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if h then
        for _, track in ipairs(h:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            track:Stop(0.2)
        end
    end
    SL.Notify("Эмоция", "Остановлено", 1.5)
end, true)

SL.PlayerTab:Toggle("🔁 Зациклить эмоцию", false, function(on)
    SL.emoteLoop = on
    if not on and SL.emoteLoopConn then
        SL.emoteLoopConn:Disconnect()
        SL.emoteLoopConn = nil
    end
end)

-- Генерируем кнопки для всех эмоций
for _, e in ipairs(SL.EMOTES) do
    local label, id = e[1], e[2]
    SL.PlayerTab:Button(label, function()
        if id:sub(1, 4) == "rbxa" or id:sub(1, 3) == "rbx" then
            -- Играем через LoadAnimation (FE — трек реплицируется)
            SL.playEmoteById(id)
            SL.Notify("Эмоция", label, 1.5)
        else
            -- Встроенная Roblox-эмоция
            local ok, err = pcall(SL.playEmoteByName, id)
            if not ok then
                -- Запасной вариант: по rbxgameasset для стандартных эмоций
                local fallback = {
                    wave      = "rbxassetid://507770239",
                    dance     = "rbxassetid://507771019",
                    dance2    = "rbxassetid://507776043",
                    dance3    = "rbxassetid://507777268",
                    laugh     = "rbxassetid://507770818",
                    cheer     = "rbxassetid://507770677",
                    applaud   = "rbxassetid://507770880",
                    cry       = "rbxassetid://507770797",
                    sleep     = "rbxassetid://323712841",
                    salute    = "rbxassetid://3360689775",
                    shrug     = "rbxassetid://3360692915",
                    think     = "rbxassetid://616158929",
                    thumbsup  = "rbxassetid://507770677",
                }
                local fbId = fallback[id]
                if fbId then
                    SL.playEmoteById(fbId)
                    SL.Notify("Эмоция", label, 1.5)
                else
                    SL.Notify("Эмоция", label .. " (/e " .. id .. ")", 2)
                end
            else
                SL.Notify("Эмоция", label, 1.5)
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Freecam: ПК — ПКМ + WASD/E/Q (Shift быстрее); телефон — джойстик + свайп по экрану + кнопки ▲▼
SL.fc = {conn = nil, touchConn = nil, speed = 60, gui = nil, up = false, down = false}
function SL.StopFreecam()
    if SL.fc.conn then SL.fc.conn:Disconnect() SL.fc.conn = nil end
    if SL.fc.touchConn then SL.fc.touchConn:Disconnect() SL.fc.touchConn = nil end
    if SL.fc.gui then SL.fc.gui:Destroy() SL.fc.gui = nil end
    SL.fc.up, SL.fc.down = false, false
    SL.UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
    local h, r = SL.GetHum(), SL.GetRoot()
    if h then cam.CameraSubject = h end
    if r then r.Anchored = false end
end
SL.UtilTab:Toggle("Freecam", false, function(on)
    SL.StopFreecam()
    if not on then return end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local pitch, yaw = cam.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
    local pos = cam.CFrame.Position
    cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
    local r0 = SL.GetRoot()
    if r0 then r0.Anchored = true end

    if SL.isMobile then
        SL.fc.gui = SL.New("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 120), ZIndex = 45,
        }, SL.ScreenGui)
        local function mk(txt, y, key)
            local b = SL.New("TextButton", {
                BackgroundColor3 = SL.Theme.Top, Text = txt, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 20,
                TextColor3 = SL.Theme.Text, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 56),
                AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 46,
            }, SL.fc.gui)
            SL.Round(b, 10)
            b.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then SL.fc[key] = true end
            end)
            b.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then SL.fc[key] = false end
            end)
        end
        mk("▲", 0, "up")
        mk("▼", 64, "down")
        SL.fc.touchConn = SL.UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i, gpe)
            if gpe then return end
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                yaw -= i.Delta.X * 0.006
                pitch = math.clamp(pitch - i.Delta.Y * 0.006, -1.5, 1.5)
            end
        end)
    end

    SL.fc.conn = SL.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        local rot = CFrame.fromEulerAnglesYXZ(pitch, yaw, 0)
        if SL.isMobile then
            local h = SL.GetHum()
            local md = h and h.MoveDirection or Vector3.zero
            local vy = (SL.fc.up and 1 or 0) - (SL.fc.down and 1 or 0)
            pos += (Vector3.new(md.X, 0, md.Z) + Vector3.new(0, vy, 0)) * SL.fc.speed * dt
        else
            if SL.UIS:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) then
                SL.UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCurrentPosition
                local d = SL.UIS:GetMouseDelta()
                yaw -= d.X * 0.004
                pitch = math.clamp(pitch - d.Y * 0.004, -1.5, 1.5)
            else
                SL.UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
            end
            rot = CFrame.fromEulerAnglesYXZ(pitch, yaw, 0)
            local mv = Vector3.zero
            if SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then mv += Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
            if SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then mv += Vector3.new(0, 0, 1) end
            if SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then mv += Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0) end
            if SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then mv += Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) end
            if SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) then mv += Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
            if SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q) then mv += Vector3.new(0, -1, 0) end
            local spd = SL.fc.speed * (SL.UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) and 3 or 1)
            if mv.Magnitude > 0 then pos += rot:VectorToWorldSpace(mv.Unit) * spd * dt end
        end
        cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos) * rot
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(SL.StopFreecam)
SL.UtilTab:Slider("Скорость Freecam", 10, 300, 60, function(v) SL.fc.speed = v end)

-- Spectate
SL.specName = ""

SL.specTg = nil
SL.specTg = SL.UtilTab:Toggle("Наблюдать за игроком", false, function(on)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not on then
        local h = SL.GetHum()
        if h then cam.CameraSubject = h end
        return
    end
    local q, target = string.lower(SL.specName), nil
    for _, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= SL.LocalPlayer and q ~= "" and (string.find(string.lower(p.Name), q, 1, true) or string.find(string.lower(p.DisplayName), q, 1, true)) then
            target = p break
        end
    end
    local th = target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if th then
        cam.CameraSubject = th
        SL.Notify("Spectate", "Наблюдаю за " .. target.Name, 2)
    else
        SL.Notify("Spectate", "Игрок не найден", 3, true)
        task.defer(function() SL.specTg:Set(false, true) end)
    end
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() local h = SL.GetHum() if h then workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = h end end)

SL.zoomSaved = nil
SL.UtilTab:Toggle("Снять лимит зума камеры", false, function(on)
    if on then
        SL.zoomSaved = {SL.LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance, SL.LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance}
        SL.LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 10000
        SL.LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0
    elseif SL.zoomSaved then
        SL.LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance, SL.LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = SL.zoomSaved[1], SL.zoomSaved[2]
        SL.zoomSaved = nil
    end
end)

SL.UtilTab:Section("Защита и производительность")
SL.avConn = nil
SL.UtilTab:Toggle("Anti-Void (возврат при падении)", false, function(on)
    if SL.avConn then SL.avConn:Disconnect() SL.avConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    local limit = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight + 60
    local lastSafe
    SL.avConn = SL.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local h, r = SL.GetHum(), SL.GetRoot()
        if not (h and r) then return end
        if h.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then lastSafe = r.CFrame end
        if lastSafe and r.Position.Y < limit then
            r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            r.CFrame = lastSafe + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
            SL.Notify("Anti-Void", "Возврат на безопасную позицию", 2)
        end
    end)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.avConn then SL.avConn:Disconnect() end end)

SL.UtilTab:Button("FPS Boost (необратимо до перезахода)", function()
    SL.Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
    pcall(function() settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01 end)
    local n = 0
    for _, o in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if o:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or o:IsA("Trail") or o:IsA("Smoke") or o:IsA("Fire") or o:IsA("Sparkles") then
            o.Enabled = false
            n += 1
        elseif o:IsA("BasePart") then
            o.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            o.CastShadow = false
        end
    end
    SL.Notify("FPS Boost", "Отключено эффектов: " .. n, 3)
end)

function SL.playSound(assetId)
    local snd = SL.New("Sound", {
        SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(assetId),
        Volume = 1, RollOffMaxDistance = 9999,
    }, workspace.CurrentCamera)
    snd:Play()
    snd.Ended:Connect(function() snd:Destroy() end)
    task.delay(10, function() if snd and snd.Parent then snd:Destroy() end end)
end

function SL.getCurrentKsId()
    if SL.ksCustomId ~= "" then return SL.ksCustomId end
    return SL.KS_LIST[SL.ksIdx] and SL.KS_LIST[SL.ksIdx].id or ""
end
function SL.getCurrentDsId()
    if SL.dsCustomId ~= "" then return SL.dsCustomId end
    return SL.DS_LIST[SL.dsIdx] and SL.DS_LIST[SL.dsIdx].id or ""
end

-- Kill Sound: срабатывает когда умирает кто-то другой (Humanoid.Died)
SL.killSoundHooked = {}
function SL.hookKillSound(char)
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or SL.killSoundHooked[hum] then return end
    SL.killSoundHooked[hum] = true
    hum.Died:Connect(function()
        if SL.ksActive then
            local id = SL.getCurrentKsId()
            if id ~= "" then SL.playSound(id) end
        end
    end)
end

SL.ksSoundConn = nil
SL.ksBtnLabel, SL.dsBtnLabel = nil, nil
SL.ksSoundToggle = nil
SL.ksSoundToggle = SL.FunTab:Toggle(SL.Lang.killSound, false, function(on)
    SL.ksActive = on
    if on then
        SL.killSoundHooked = {}
        for _, p in ipairs(SL.Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= SL.LocalPlayer and p.Character then SL.hookKillSound(p.Character) end
            p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c) SL.hookKillSound(c) end)
        end
        if SL.ksSoundConn then SL.ksSoundConn:Disconnect() end
        SL.ksSoundConn = SL.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
            p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c) SL.hookKillSound(c) end)
        end)
        SL.Notify(SL.Lang.killSound, SL.Lang.soundOn, 1.5)
    else
        SL.Notify(SL.Lang.killSound, SL.Lang.soundOff, 1.5)
    end
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.ksSoundConn then SL.ksSoundConn:Disconnect() end SL.ksActive = false end)

    SL.ksCustomId = ""
    SL.ksBtnRef.Text = SL.Lang.killSound .. ": " .. SL.KS_LIST[SL.ksIdx].name .. " (нажми для смены)"
end, true)

    SL.ksBtnRef.Text = SL.Lang.killSound .. ": Custom (" .. t:sub(1,14) .. ")"
end)

-- Death Sound: срабатывает когда умирает локальный игрок
SL.dsSoundToggle = nil
SL.dsSoundToggle = SL.FunTab:Toggle(SL.Lang.deathSound, false, function(on)
    SL.dsActive = on
    if SL.dsCharConn then SL.dsCharConn:Disconnect() SL.dsCharConn = nil end
    if not on then SL.Notify(SL.Lang.deathSound, SL.Lang.soundOff, 1.5) return end
    local function hookDeath(char)
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end
        hum.Died:Connect(function()
            if SL.dsActive then
                local id = SL.getCurrentDsId()
                if id ~= "" then
                    task.delay(0.15, function() SL.playSound(id) end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    if SL.LocalPlayer.Character then hookDeath(SL.LocalPlayer.Character) end
    SL.dsCharConn = SL.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(hookDeath)
    SL.Notify(SL.Lang.deathSound, SL.Lang.soundOn, 1.5)
end)
SL.OnUnload(function() if SL.dsCharConn then SL.dsCharConn:Disconnect() end SL.dsActive = false end)

SL.dsBtnRef = nil
SL.dsBtnRef = SL.FunTab:Button(SL.Lang.deathSound .. ": " .. SL.DS_LIST[1].name .. " (нажми для смены)", function()
    SL.dsIdx = SL.dsIdx % #SL.DS_LIST + 1
    SL.dsCustomId = ""
    SL.dsBtnRef.Text = SL.Lang.deathSound .. ": " .. SL.DS_LIST[SL.dsIdx].name .. " (нажми для смены)"
end, true)

SL.FunTab:Input("Death Sound — свой Asset ID", "92076037937225", function(t)
    SL.dsCustomId = t
    SL.dsBtnRef.Text = SL.Lang.deathSound .. ": Custom (" .. t:sub(1,14) .. ")"
end)

SL.FunTab:Button("▶ Тест Death Sound", function()
    local id = SL.getCurrentDsId()
    if id == "" then error("выбери звук", 0) end
    SL.playSound(id)
end)
